## Section 1 — Identity Baseline (B)

**Full Name:** Hercules
**Aliases / Titles / Epithets:** Son of Zeus; formerly of Olympus; additional titles and epithets not yet established — to be defined in prose; former Olympus-era titles stripped upon banishment (LEDGER ONLY except the word Olympus, which Law P30-L8 permits in speech and thought; Son of Zeus and every other origin title stay out of prose)
**Age at Story Start:** 28
**Age at Phase 1 End-State (Part 30, Section 1.3.1a — Locked Canon):** see Section 9 (PHASE 1 END-STATE AGE block)
**Gender:** Male
**Species:** Human
**Cultural Origin (CPT per Part 25):** Formerly Olympus / Empire of Gods; currently lost in Sol Star System near the border village between Azure Sky Kingdom and neighboring kingdoms, Nine Suns Continent, Saha
**Physical Description (key identifiers):** Not yet established — to be defined in prose; son of Zeus implies exceptional physical presence. *(First-use facts, Chapter 002 (Ref: 1.1.1.002): the page gives his carriage and his work and no face. He is a heavy man who gives every rope and load what a strong man gives. He draws the well rope hand over hand, six pulls and a pause before the seventh, and carries a full bucket over the paving at the pace of a man who means to spill nothing. In a crowd he goes slowly, shoulders a little turned, as a man wades among children. He runs "as hard as a heavy man runs and no harder". With the heaviest sack on his shoulder his shoulders loosen as another man's might in a chair. His face, hair, exact height and weight and age-look are not given and stay open (OPEN-AREA). The text revised in place on 2026-10-17 adds nothing to his looks; what it adds about his power, his sense and the flicker watch is recorded in Section 3.)* *(Chapter 008 (Ref: 1.1.1.008), 2026-10-22: the page adds one fact of carriage and nothing to his face, hair, height or age-look, which stay OPEN-AREA. Through the day he keeps his shoulders "a hand narrower" and his breath at "a strong man's share"; in the unwatched hour "The shoulders he had kept a hand narrower since dawn came down, and the breath that had taken a strong man's share all day took what it wanted", and he sits on a flat stone with his hands, "which had held something since before light", open on his knees.)*
**Voice Register (per Part 29):** **REGISTERED (author-set).** A banished Demigod living as a village laborer under a personal suppression, who does not argue and does not ask, and whose whole speech register is built out of what he has decided not to spend. The eight identity-content determinants of §4.2 land on him as follows: **realm and affinity** (Demigod Level 1 Mid actual; Champion Level 8 High effective under the **Olympus Suppression Seal**, the operative personal cap per the item B closure; Sovereignty — Strength, Level 8, registered as BEARING rather than striking, `B39-L24-AFFIN-99`) give him a body that finishes a matter without a sentence, and an entire tier of realm vocabulary that is his by right and is spent in one room only (§2, **Permitted External Realm References**); **cultural origin** gives him a court's precedence instinct calibrated to the wrong civilization (`B39-L20-PREC-99`), forms and oath-forms left behind with the titles (`B39-L18-FORM-99`, `B39-L05-RITUAL-99`), and one small lexicon of ordinary words with nowhere to go but one hour a day; **ideology and social position** give him no rank in this world at all, a permanent guest on everyone's ground (11.5, `B39-L19-GUEST-99`), a man who obeys persons and never institutions (`B39-L06-RULE-99`, `B39-L13-TRUST-99`), and the cover's deference performed exactly over a standard he has never named (`B39-L13-UP-99`); **psychology** gives him feeling that arrives after the task is done and then goes into labor rather than into words (`B39-L03-ONSET-99`, `B39-L03-CHANNEL-99`), and one thought he will not let run to its end (`B39-L07-FORBID-99`); **relationship context** gives him exactly one room in which the counting stops, and one subject that stays walled even there (11.4a, `B39-L23-ROUTINE-99`, `B39-L14-FORBIDDEN-99`); **emotional state** does not reach the sentence at all, because every emotion presents as the same calm usefulness and the true reading is in the hands and their rate (`B39-L03-MASK-99`, `B39-L03-LEAK-99`); **information state** is a man whose year here is built entirely out of true sentences, who lies only by omission and knows it (`B39-L09-CAPACITY-99`, `B39-L09-MECH-99`). The register that results is **the sentence that will not move** — one plain statement, said again unchanged if it is pushed, and past that a silence with a man standing in it.

**Prosodic Signatures (Rule 29.4.4.1):** Four determinants from the Prosodic Signature Menu are registered — **item 4** (question habit, author-locked), **item 1** (sentence-length distribution, author-locked), **item 5** (hedging versus bluntness, `[DERIVED]` and flagged for author confirmation) and **item 7** (technical-register default, **ruled by the author**: the cast-wide trigger is `` `[ASKED]` `` and the drop-depth is `` `[DERIVED]` `` and flagged as a proposal for author confirmation at Check #21) — plus **item 6** (epigram appetite) **RULED NOT EPIGRAMMATIC AT ZERO by explicit author ruling** `[ASKED]`, one §4.2 identity-content element deliberately **not** claimed as a menu determinant, and one registered contextual register break. Menu items 2, 3, 8 and 9 are recorded as unregistered by choice. **Rule 29.4.4.1's floor of two is discharged by element 2 (item 4) and element 4 (item 1), both author-locked; element 5's ruled zero and element 6's derived status therefore do not bear on the floor.** Each element below is anchored to a registered Part 39 dimension, cited and not altered.

> ### THE CONCEALMENT LAW AND THE P30-L8 RAIL GOVERN EVERY VOCABULARY CHOICE ON THIS PAGE. Read before drafting a line of his speech.
> §2's **Permitted External Realm References** block is Locked Canon and it is a *permission*, not a habit. Its own narrative conditions state the default: **Hercules conceals this vocabulary from in-system interlocutors, and the narration follows his concealment.** `B39-L09-CONCEAL-99` and `B39-L13-MASK-99` register the same thing as conduct. **His speech may never carry realm vocabulary above the local ceiling in any ordinary scene**, and the permission is live in exactly three places: his interior, a self-identity statement, and dialogue with an external-exposure character. In Phase 1 practice that is the unwatched hour (11.4a) and nothing else.
> **Where that permission and the P30-L8 rail meet, stated exactly, because a drafting agent will need this written down.** The two do not overlap and they are not the same kind of thing. **§2 grants a vocabulary of REALMS** — Demigod, Void God, Ruler, Monarch, Core Formation and the rest are words for cultivation stages, and they are admissible where §2 says they are. **P30-L8 forbids an ENTITY** — a polity with a name, a history and a referent behind it, forbidden in Phase 1 prose in every form including interior monologue. An entity is not a realm word, and no realm word is an entity. **Therefore:** a permitted realm word spoken inside §2's conditions is not a P30-L8 event and never becomes one; and the forbidden entity may not appear anywhere on the page at all, **including inside the interior memory that §2 admits (the word Olympus, the Seal, the reasons for the banishment and the promise to clear his name; never the Empire of Gods or any other name for the origin).** §11's header already states the resolution and this field only restates it: every L34 origin cell naming the fall is **ledger-internal and legal**, Part 39 provenance lives in Section 11, and it never reaches prose. **The clean formulation: §2's permission regarding the entity is a LEDGER permission; §2's permission regarding realm vocabulary is a PROSE permission bounded by three conditions.** Where the two bindings touch, **the tighter binding governs** — the same principle the author applied to the Lock and the Seal at item B, reaching by a second route the place §11's header already stands. `[DERIVED]`
> **The Olympus Suppression Seal is ledger vocabulary.** Item B is closed and the Seal is his operative personal cap, which is what the pressure presses on and what Phase 1 cracks; the Firmament Lock is the outer system ceiling and is not what he presses on. **The Seal is named in this field and in Section 11, and may be named in speech and thought in Phase 1 prose under the P30-L8 exception.** Item B is used here and is not reopened.

**1. THE PLANTING IN SPEECH — two mechanisms, both author-locked. Registered as a §4.2 Social Position and Information State element, and deliberately NOT claimed as one of the nine menu determinants.** `[ASKED]`
**Anchor:** `B39-L08-REFLEX-99` `[SIGNATURE]` `[IMMUTABLE]` — *THE PLANTING: he puts his body between a thing and whoever it would fall on — a collapsing beam, an angry man, an accusation aimed at someone small — and once his feet are set the matter is finished; **he does not argue with people; he stands somewhere, and the standing is the argument**.* That clause is the whole of this element, carried from conduct into speech.

**(a) HE REPEATS IT, UNCHANGED.** `[ASKED]`
He states his position **once**. Contradicted, he says **the identical sentence again, word for word.** No rephrasing. No added reasoning. No escalation. Then nothing. **No rebuttal exists in his register at all** — there is no sentence anywhere in which he addresses the content of the objection, tells the other person they are wrong, or supplies a second argument. **There is nothing to argue against, which is exactly why the standing wins.** The room does not lose to him; the room runs out of things to push against.
- **THE COUNTING TEST, for Check #19 and for every drafting agent. Six properties, each countable on the page.** `[ASKED]`
  1. **One statement.** The position is given once, plain, in the default short register (element 4).
  2. **Verbatim, not paraphrase.** The second utterance is **word for word identical** to the first. Not a shortened form, not a summary, not the same idea in other words. **An auditor can run this by comparing the two strings**, and they must match.
  3. **No reasoning added.** Nothing between the two utterances explains, justifies or supports the first. No *because*, no evidence, no appeal.
  4. **No escalation.** No raised register, no intensifier, no consequence named, no threat, no volume. The second delivery is the same size as the first.
  5. **No third form.** He does not produce a variant afterward. What follows the repetition is silence and a man who has not moved (`B39-L13-BOUND-99`, `B39-L15-DISSENT-99`).
  6. **No rebuttal, anywhere.** The register contains no counter-argument construction at all.
- **BINDING ON THE DRAFTING AGENT.** **A drafting agent who has him rephrase has broken this element. A drafting agent who has him elaborate has broken this element.** A better second sentence is not an improvement here; it is the failure. If a scene seems to require him to explain himself, the scene requires a different character to explain it, or it requires him to say nothing.
- **RECONCILIATION WITH P29-9, THE PROHIBITION ON REPETITIVE DIALOGUE. Checked, and it is the most important reconciliation in this element.** `[DERIVED]` P29-9 forbids **stasis** — exchanges that cover the same ground *without advancement*. The verbatim repetition is not stasis, because what advances is not the sentence but the room: the first utterance is a statement, the second is a refusal to negotiate, and P29-9's own list of what counts as advancement includes **new social configuration** and **new understanding of who these characters are and what is between them**. The identical second sentence delivers both. Three limits keep it lawful and they are binding:
  1. **At most twice per exchange.** Once stated, once repeated. **A third identical utterance is stasis and is a P29-9 failure.**
  2. **The repetition must answer a genuine contradiction**, not a silence and not a request for clarification. Without a push there is nothing for the repetition to refuse.
  3. **The surrounding prose carries the advancement** (Rule 29.2.1.2 embedded mandate, P29-10): the room's reaction, the other speakers' positions, the physical fact of a man who has not moved. **The repeated line is never a dialogue-only line.**
- **Where the immovability comes from, cited not altered.** `B39-L06-SPEED-99` (decides slowly and finally; revisiting is rare — **there is nothing to revise, because the deciding finished before the speaking started**), `B39-L15-DISSENT-99` (persuaded, he moves; unpersuaded, he does not), `B39-L13-BOUND-99` (the question he will not answer meets a man who has stopped moving), `B39-L08-GAP-99` (he states almost nothing; the operative ethic is fully visible in conduct and the gap is silence, not hypocrisy), `B39-L15-ANNOUNCE-99` (decisions are announced by his feet), `B39-L12-CLOSE-99` (closes with an act, not a word).
- **Boundary with Layer L10, stated so neither registry is double-counted.** `[DERIVED]` `B39-L10-REFUSE-99` registers the **refusal as conduct** — positional, the matter finished where his feet are set — and refusal form is expressly **Part 39 Layer L10** under the §4.4 boundary paragraph, audited at Check #20. **This element registers what the sentence does when he speaks at all**, which is the Part 29 side, audited at Check #19. Both are cited, neither is altered, and **neither substitutes for the other.**

**(b) HE PUTS ONE FLAT QUESTION TO THE ROOM.** `[ASKED]`
**Anchor:** `B39-L08-POWERLESS-99` `[SIGNATURE]` — *THE HEARING: against unfairness he interposes bodily — stands beside the accused in front of everyone, saying almost nothing — AND insists, quietly and past comfort, that the accused be allowed to answer, that someone check; he gives every person the hearing he never got.* *(Author ruling, 2026-10-21: "the hearing he never got" is read as a hearing that would have weighed his answer and checked the evidence; he and Bison spoke against the false charge and were not believed. The registered conduct is unchanged; see the second exception block at 11.4a. The other mentions of it in this Section are read the same way.)*
**Not to his opponent. To everyone present.** *Has anyone checked. Did anyone see it. Was he asked.* And then he lets the room answer itself.
- **THE COUNTING TEST. Four properties.** `[ASKED]`
  1. **It is addressed to nobody in particular.** He does not name a person to answer it and does not turn to face the accuser.
  2. **It is procedural.** It asks whether a thing was done — checked, seen, asked — and never what a person deserves, never what the truth is, never what anyone thinks.
  3. **It is one.** He does not follow it up, does not rephrase it into a sharper form, and does not press an individual who fails to answer.
  4. **He does not speak again until somebody else has.** The silence after the question is his, and it is doing the work.
- **This is element 2's FREE class spending itself at its highest value**, and it is the one place in his life where he asks something instantly and without arithmetic (`B39-L06-RISK-99`: *he spends recognition-risk like a miser, except when something is falling on someone, where he spends it all at once*).
- **The involuntary edge, cited not altered.** `B39-L03-UNFAKE-99` registers that he **cannot perform indifference to an accusation landing in his hearing** — everything else he can hide, and that one stands him up; `B39-L26-INSULT-99` registers that the room feels the temperature change. **Even here no rebuttal appears.** What changes is that he speaks at all, and that the flatness has heat behind it. A drafting agent who converts that heat into an argument has broken element 1(a) in the same line.
- **Conduct twins, cited and not re-registered.** `B39-L10-INTERRUPT-99` (almost never with his voice; when words are about to become harm, his body interrupts before his mouth does), `B39-L09-OTHERS-99` (being lied to gets the hearing instinct, not the pounce: he checks), `B39-L18-BREAKER-99` (an accused oath-breaker gets the hearing first), `B39-L13-BETRAY-99` (betrayal gets the proceeding he never got). All are Part 39 conduct at Check #20 and none is re-registered here.

**2. THE QUESTION ECONOMY (menu item 4, question habit) — three rules running together, all author-locked.** `[ASKED]` *(See Evolution Entry 3, Section 14: by the author's own ruling of 2026-10-07 the NEVER class below no longer holds in daily village talk and work, and the ruling touches the immutable `B39-L06-INFO-99`. The text of this element is the base and is not edited.)*
**Anchors:** `B39-L06-INFO-99` `[SIGNATURE]` `[IMMUTABLE]` — *THE WATCHING: he learns by watching people's hands ... **asking is dangerous (E11, E16), so the eyes do the asking**;* and `B39-L29-WATCH-99` `[SIGNATURE]` — *HE WATCHES THE CRAFT ... **the only hobby a man who cannot ask questions can have**.*
**Menu-slotting note.** `[DERIVED]` The **value** below is author-locked at the interview and is `[ASKED]` in every particular. The **slotting of that value into menu item 4** is a registration act recorded here, made because Rule 29.4.4.1's item 4 asks precisely *whether the speaker advances by asking, and what their questions do*, and the locked material answers it in full and in three parts. If the author rules the slotting away, **the value stands unchanged as a §4.2 element and the floor of two is still discharged by elements 4 and 5**, so nothing in this field depends on it.

- **FREE — procedural, and on someone else's behalf.** `[ASKED]` *Has anyone checked. Was he asked. Who is carrying this.* **Unlimited, and instant.** These reveal nothing whatever about him: a question about how a third party was treated carries no information about the asker's origin, competence or knowledge. **Registered evidence, cited not altered:** `B39-L15-RECV-99` — *reported to (rare), he listens fully and asks one thing: who is carrying the result* — is a registered question and is exactly this class; `B39-L09-OTHERS-99` (he checks) and `B39-L08-POWERLESS-99` (that someone check) are the same class in their two commonest settings.
- **COSTLY — anything that reveals he does not know how this world works.** `[ASKED]` **A man ignorant of ordinary things is a man from somewhere else**, and that is the entire danger: not the subject of the question but the fact of needing to ask it. **He does ask these. Rarely. And every single time it is a calculated exposure**, priced before it is spoken and paid afterward.
  - **THE RENDERING RULE — the reader must be able to see him decide to spend one.** `[ASKED]` `[DERIVED]` Three beats, in order, and all three are countable: **(i)** a pause before the question that is longer than the question — `B39-L36-TEXTURE-99` registers *the pause before his feet set*, and this is the same pause in speech; **(ii)** the question itself, plain, small, and as narrow as the task allows, because a wide question exposes more surface; **(iii)** the payment, which is that he watches the answerer while they answer, reading what the asking has just told them (`B39-L02-BLIND-99`, `B39-L06-INFO-99`). **A costly question written without the pause and without the watching has been written as a free one, and the element has been lost.**
  - **The engine, cited not altered.** `B39-L36-COST-99` registers that **he cannot stop learning — the appetite for how things work pulls him where a man in hiding should not linger, and he gets remembered.** The costly question is that registered appetite arriving in words instead of in loitering. `B39-L09-DISCLOSE-99` supplies the grammar: exposure exists **only as a chosen act, never as a leak.**
  - **The dividing line, stated so it can be applied.** `[DERIVED]` **He will not spend a question on wanting to know. He will spend one on needing to do.** A question that serves a task in front of him, or a person in front of him, can be bought. A question that serves his own curiosity cannot, and that is the next rule.
- **NEVER — on his own behalf, outside the unwatched hour.** `[ASKED]` Zero. Not about the world, not about himself, not about what he wants to understand. **Registered evidence, cited not altered:** `B39-L22-NAV-99` (*landmarks collected by observation, roads learned by walking them, nothing asked*), `B39-L24-LEARN-99` (*asking is dangerous, so the eyes do the asking*), `B39-L29-WATCH-99` (*the only hobby a man who cannot ask questions can have*). **These three are the NEVER class stated three times in the register already.**

> ### THE RECONCILIATION. This is the heart of the register and it is written out in full because THE WATCHING is immutable and must survive intact.
> **The apparent problem.** `B39-L06-INFO-99` is immutable and its registered origin says the concealment law **makes asking dangerous**, which is why the eyes do the asking. This element then has him asking in three places. Read carelessly, the element revises an immutable Signature. It does not.
> **The resolution: asking is expensive, not impossible.** THE WATCHING is a statement about **what a question costs him**, not about whether his mouth can form one. **A miser is not a man without money.** The dangerous class is specific and identifiable — a question that reveals ignorance of ordinary things marks the asker as from somewhere else — and every one of the three places above is either outside that class or inside it and knowingly paid for.
> **Place one, the free question, does not touch THE WATCHING at all.** THE WATCHING governs the acquisition of ordinary competence, and **none of that competence is ever acquired by asking**: he still learns the loom, the knot and the latch from hands, one demonstration behind, exactly as registered. A procedural question about how a third party was treated acquires no competence and reveals no ignorance. **It is not the dangerous class, so it costs nothing, so the miser spends it freely.**
> **Place two, the costly question, is THE WATCHING's price list rather than its contradiction.** The registered clause says the eyes do the asking **because the mouth is expensive.** A rare, deliberate, paid-for question is what expensive means. `B39-L06-RISK-99` states the same economy in the register's own words: *he spends recognition-risk like a miser, except when something is falling on someone, where he spends it all at once.* **The miser clause and the exception clause are this element's COSTLY and FREE classes, already registered.**
> **Place three, the unwatched hour, is where the stored balance goes** (element 3). It is the one hour in which a question carries no cost at all, and it is the only channel his own curiosity has ever had.
> **What the reconciliation actually says about the man, recorded plainly and without softening.** `[ASKED]` **He demands for others exactly what he has denied himself for a year.** He will not spend one question on wanting to understand the world he lives in, and he will spend one instantly, in front of everyone, to get a stranger the chance to answer a charge. `B39-L08-POWERLESS-99` names it from the other side: *he gives every person the hearing he never got.* **The asymmetry is not a flaw in the economy; it is the economy.** A drafting agent who writes him asking freely on his own account has not softened him, but has erased both Signatures at once.
> **THE WATCHING survives intact.** Nothing above adds a word to it, moves it, or qualifies it, and no dimension line is altered anywhere.

**3. REGISTER BREAK — THE UNWATCHED HOUR. Three changes, all author-locked. A registered contextual register under Rule 29.4.1.2.** `[ASKED]`
**Anchor:** `B39-L23-ROUTINE-99` `[SIGNATURE]` — *THE UNWATCHED HOUR: every day has a stretch where he and Bison are alone and he stops performing — speaks the old vocabulary, names what he is, says what the concealment law forbids everywhere else; **the only hour of his day in which he is not lying by omission**; guarded by both of them without ever having been agreed.* Cross-references: 11.4a **The Bison System**, 11.4 row *The unwatched hour*, `B39-L09-MECH-99`, `B39-L21-AFTER-99`, `B39-L19-PLACE-99`.

**(i) HE ASKS.** `[ASKED]` It is the one hour in which a question is safe, and he spends it asking things. **A year of stored curiosity, one hour a day, only ever of one person.** This is element 2's NEVER class standing down, and it is the only place it ever does.
- **THE CONSEQUENCE, RECORDED FLATLY.** `[ASKED]` **The day the hour ends, he stops asking altogether.**
- **The operative reading of that sentence, bounded so it cannot be misapplied.** `[DERIVED]` What ends is **asking as a thing he does for himself** — the whole of the class the hour exists to serve. **The FREE class continues**, because those questions were never his: `B39-L15-RECV-99` and `B39-L08-POWERLESS-99` are registered conduct and are not touched by the loss. **The COSTLY class continues to exist and to be almost never spent.** The line the author locked describes the class the hour carried, and it is absolute within that class.
- **Governance, stated so no auditor reads it as an unregistered drift.** `[DERIVED]` This is the Part 29 face of **drift map row 6** (loss of Bison — THE CARRYING TURNS COMPULSIVE) and of `B39-L23-ROUTINE-99`'s own registered `L34-END-03`, *the loss of the person it exists with.* **No drift row is added and no dimension is moved.** The register break simply ends when its anchor ends, which is already governed.

**(ii) THE LENGTH OPENS UP.** `[ASKED]` The clipped register goes. **He talks properly, at length, the way a person talks who is not counting the cost of every sentence.** Element 4's short default is arithmetic and not temperament, and this is the hour in which the arithmetic stops.
- **BINDING SEPARATION FROM ELEMENT 4'S LONG REGISTER, and a drafting agent will confuse the two.** `[DERIVED]` **They have different triggers and different contents.** Element 4's long register fires on **a task in hand** and is long **about the work and nothing else**. This one fires on **the hour** and is long **about anything**, because the cost has stood down rather than because the subject is safe. **Neither may be imported into the other**, and length that arrives in a village scene without a task in hand has been written from the wrong register.

**(iii) THE OLD VOCABULARY RETURNS — TIGHTLY BOUNDED. See the rail guard below, which is binding.** `[ASKED]`

> ### ⚠️ RAIL GUARD — THE OLD VOCABULARY. THIS IS THE HIGHEST RAIL RISK IN HIS REGISTER. BUILD IT WITH EXTREME CARE.
> **The rail, as the §11 header states it (Locked, P30-L8).** The polity of his origin is a **FORBIDDEN ENTITY** in Phase 1 prose: **never named, mentioned, depicted, referenced, or alluded to, in any form, dialogue and interior monologue included.** **The §11 header carries the rail in its own words and is the citation for it; this field deliberately does not repeat the name and calls that polity the forbidden entity throughout.** The same treatment covers every other name for that polity; the word Olympus, the Seal, the reasons for the banishment and the promise to clear his name are the P30-L8 exception and may be spoken or thought.
> **TWO LEXICONS OPEN IN THE HOUR, AND THIS GUARD GOVERNS ONLY THE SECOND. Stated first, because everything else depends on the routing.** `[DERIVED]`
> - **Lexicon A — the §2 realm designations.** Demigod, Void God, Ruler, Monarch, Core Formation and the rest. These are **cultivation-stage words**, they are Locked-Canon permitted inside the hour by §2's narrative conditions, and `B39-L07-SELF-99` records the canonical self-identity statement built from them. **They travel under §2's permit and under §2's conditions, and this rail guard does not narrow them.**
> - **Lexicon B — the old words for ordinary things.** The tool, the technique, the physical state, the quality of light. **This is what the author's lock governs, and its permit is closed.**
> **AUTHOR LOCK ON WHAT MAY REACH THE PAGE. A closed permit list. Nothing outside it exists.** `[ASKED]` An old word may name:
>   1. **A tool** — a thing that could be set on a bench.
>   2. **A technique** — a thing done with the hands.
>   3. **A physical state** — a thing felt in the body.
>   4. **A quality of light** — a thing seen in the air.
>   5. **That is the entire list.** The permit exists **only for things this world has no name for**, and it exists for nothing else.
> **WHAT AN OLD WORD MAY NEVER NAME. Concrete and exhaustive; each entry is independently disqualifying.** `[ASKED]`
>   1. **Never a place** — no city, hall, ground, mountain, region, direction or landscape of origin, named or described.
>   2. **Never a person** — no name from before, no epithet, and no kin term standing in for one. `B39-L14-FORBIDDEN-99` and canon decision 7 bear directly: the father is a standard and never a word. *(Author ruling, 2026-10-21: this entry governs old words only, the Lexicon B permit. The plain reference "my father" that the Chapter 8 hour opens is not an old word and does not pass through this permit; see the second exception block at 11.4a.)*
>   3. **Never an institution** — no court, council, order, house, guard, school, army or office.
>   4. **Never a title** — no rank, honorific or style of address from before, **his own included**; the titles were stripped (§1, `B39-L10-NAME-99`, `B39-L18-FORM-99`).
>   5. **Never a realm designation** — not because realm words are forbidden, but because **they are Lexicon A**: realm vocabulary travels under §2's permit and under §2's conditions and is never smuggled in as an old word for an ordinary thing. This is a routing rule, not a narrowing of §2.
>   6. **Never the forbidden entity, in any form** — no polity, no people, no sky, no periphrasis standing in for one. **P30-L8 is absolute and unconditional and applies inside the hour exactly as it applies outside it.**
>   7. **Never an event of the fall** — no proceeding, charge, trial, verdict, banishment, betrayal or death. This is `B39-L14-FORBIDDEN-99`'s **UNBEARABLE SUBJECT** and canon decisions 1 and 7, **walled even in the one room where speech is legal**: *he may say what he IS; he may not say what was DONE.* *(Author ruling, 2026-10-21: this entry governs old words only. The plain-tongue outline of the fall that the Chapter 8 hour opens (a betrayal, a false charge for a crime not committed, that they spoke against it and were not believed, the exile) is not an old word and does not pass through this permit; the crime, the charge, the victim or death and the detail of the proceeding stay barred. See the second exception block at 11.4a.)*
>   8. **Never a word that would require a gloss to be understood** — a gloss is an explanation, and an explanation is a route to every item above.
> **THE MECHANICAL RULE THAT MAKES THE GUARD ENFORCEABLE — THE REFERENT TEST.** `[DERIVED]` **An old word may point only at a physical object, a physical action, a bodily state, or a sensory quality. If its referent is none of those four, the word does not exist.** This is the single check an auditor can run in one pass, and it is the reason the slide is impossible: **every route to the rail runs through a referent that is none of the four.** A polity is not an object. A proceeding is not an action done with the hands. A title is not a bodily state. A homeland is not a quality of light. **The permit is a referent test rather than a word test**, and it is applied by asking what the word points at.
> **THE VOCABULARY APPEARS AND IS ENTIRELY INERT AS EVIDENCE.** `[ASKED]` A listener who overheard the whole hour would learn that two men have private words for a chisel and for a certain slant of afternoon light. **There is no chain from that to a place, a people, or a fall, because the permit contains no referent from which such a chain could start.** The safety and the warmth are the same property.
> **THE INTIMACY THE LOCK PRODUCES, recorded because it is the point of the element and not a side effect.** `[ASKED]` **Two exiles using their own words for ordinary things, because ordinary things are what they miss.** Not the titles and not the sky. The tool, the grip, the light at a particular hour. **A drafting agent who reaches past the permit is not only breaking the rail; they are writing the wrong feeling.** The prose instinct and the canon rail point the same way here, which is what makes the element safe to hand over.
> **OPEN CANON — flagged, not filled.** `[DERIVED]` **This element requires old vocabulary on the page, and canon supplies none.** No word of Lexicon B is established anywhere in canon, **the language has no established name**, and §11 registers no lexicon for him: `B39-L18-FORM-99` records that whatever he once swore by was left with the titles, `B39-L05-RITUAL-99` that nothing at his table names its origin, and `B39-L16-TRANSMIT-99` that nothing transmissible surfaces his origin. **Therefore: no drafting agent may coin an old word, and no drafting agent may name the language.** Until an author ruling supplies vocabulary, **the lawful rendering is an untranslated utterance the narration does not spell out and does not gloss**, or narration recording that a word from before was used without reproducing it. **Author ruling required** before any prose puts a spelled old word in his mouth. Part 28 Area 20's Entity Naming Law and Gate 1 Check-17 bear on any such coinage.

> **Rule 29.4.1.2 handling, stated so no auditor reads it as a consistency failure.** This is a **registered contextual register**, not an arbitrary scene-to-scene shift. Rule 29.4.1.2 prohibits voice that moves without governance; it does not prohibit a register written down in advance, bound to one named person, given a stated firing condition and a stated boundary, and reciprocally recorded on the Part 39 side at `B39-L23-ROUTINE-99`, `B39-L14-DELTA-99` and `B39-L14-WITNESS-99`. The break fires in **exactly one context**, at **exactly three stated determinantal changes**, and reverts at a stated moment: **the presence of any third person**, where `B39-L14-WITNESS-99` registers that in any in-system company the concealment resumes for both and the friendship visibly reduces to its cover version. **No governed voice-change event under Rules 29.4.1.2 or 29.4.1.3 has occurred or is claimed.** An auditor finding this on the page has found the register working.
> **Bison is named here because the author-locked material requires the naming and for no other reason.** `[DERIVED]` **No conduct is registered for him by this field, no dimension or delta is created in his file, no cell is added to anybody's Layer L14 matrix, and no comparison, complementarity or contrast with his register or with any other is registered or implied.** Per the standing author ruling.

*First shown on a page at 1.1.1.008 (2026-10-22), the register break of the unwatched hour, with Bison alone after his sense had found no one within sight or hearing. (i) He asks: five questions, all inside the hour and none outside it ("What was in it?", "Where did you go that night, Bison?", "And after you had walked the ground?", "Do you want it said?", "Why does the end go back under itself like that?"). (ii) The length opens, in full connected sentences, and the page marks it ("His voice came out at its full length", "His voice was his ordinary voice"). (iii) No old word is spoken, spelled, coined or glossed: the author ruled none for Part 1 and the closed permit list stands. Nothing in the Chapter shows the break ending. The wall of the hour and its two exceptions are at 11.4a; the Check #21 rulings are Evolution Entries 9 to 12, Section 14.*

**4. Sentence-length distribution (menu item 1) — A STATED MIX WITH A STATED TRIGGER.** `[ASKED]` *(See Evolution Entry 3, Section 14: by the author's ruling of 2026-10-07 his turns run longer than the short default in daily village talk and work. The text of this element is the base and is not edited.)*
- **DEFAULT: SHORT.** **The fewest words that will do.** Not terseness of temperament and not gruffness: it is the concealment doing arithmetic. Every additional sentence is additional surface, and surface is where a man who is not from here gets read. **Anchors:** `B39-L08-GAP-99` (*he states almost nothing*), `B39-L10-TURN-99` (yields turns easily; speaks last, if at all), `B39-L10-SILENCE-99` (silence load-bearing and comfortable — *the resting state of a man who cannot volunteer*), `B39-L09-MECH-99` (*he never states a falsehood; he never finishes the truth* — a short sentence has less to leave unfinished).
- **MARKED: LONG.** He will **describe a task in front of him in full** — the method, the order, the weight, what will hold and what will not — **because describing work is safe.** A sentence about a beam contains no information about the man. **Anchors:** `B39-L03-CHANNEL-99` `[SIGNATURE]` (INTO WORK — feeling converts immediately to labor; the labor is what he has instead of expression), `B39-L29-WATCH-99` `[SIGNATURE]` (the craft attended for hours, the accumulated stock the long register draws on), `B39-L07-MODE-99` (thinks in loads and methods — the long register is that interior reaching speech), `B39-L07-COUNT-99` (counts loads and increments), `B39-L16-TEACH-99` (works alongside and lets the learner watch his hands), `B39-L10-PRAISE-99` (praise is exact and aimed at craft — the same register at its shortest).
- **THE TRIGGER IS A TASK IN HAND, AND IT IS CHECKABLE. Two conditions, both required.** `[ASKED]`
  1. **A physical task is present in the scene** — in his hands, in front of him, or about to be begun. **Not a remembered task and not a hypothetical one.**
  2. **The task is the subject he is addressing.** The moment the subject leaves the work, the length collapses to the default in the same breath.
- **THE NEAR-MISS THAT INVERTS IT, and it is the trap most likely to be walked into.** `[DERIVED]` `B39-L09-TELL-99` registers that **a question that comes too close gets answered with work — his body moves toward a task and the subject changes with it.** That produces a task in hand, and it does **not** open the long register. **It closes him.** Condition 2 is what separates them: in the deflection the task is the exit rather than the subject, and he performs it while saying the minimum or nothing. **The countable form: after a question that comes too close, his word count goes DOWN.** A drafting agent who lets a deflection bloom into a full description of the work has written the register backward.
- **THE LONG REGISTER OPENS NO OTHER DOOR. BINDING.** `[ASKED]` `[DERIVED]` What opens is **the length**, and nothing else. **It does not raise the vocabulary ceiling**, it does not admit §2 realm vocabulary (the concealment default holds, per the boxed note at the head of this field), it does not admit Lexicon B (element 3's permit is bound to the hour), and **it does not extend from the work to the man.** Where the subject would cross from the task to himself, the register is already the default short.
- **NOT A REGISTER BREAK, AND NOT AN OFF-REGISTER ACT.** `[DERIVED]` The long register is **in register**: Rule 29.4.4.1 expressly provides for *a stated mix with a stated trigger*, and the trigger is stated and countable above. **It does not consume the `B39-L36-OFFREG-99` allowance**, which is registered as occurring **only inside the unwatched hour, before Bison**, marked by Bison's reaction, at most one per Section under Rule 39.40.2.4. **It is also not element 3**, which is bound to the hour and is long about anything rather than long about the work.
- **OVERLAP NOTE, recorded as information and not as a comparison.** `[DERIVED]` **Another character in this cast holds a similarly work-triggered length change.** The author was shown this and accepted it. Per the standing ruling, overlaps between registers are permitted and **no distinctness check runs at derivation time.** **No character is named here, no comparison is drawn, and none may be added to this field.**

**5. Epigram appetite (menu item 6) — ZERO. MARKED NOT EPIGRAMMATIC BY EXPLICIT AUTHOR RULING.** `[ASKED]`
**The author has ruled: he is NOT EPIGRAMMATIC, the budget is ZERO, and the zero rests on the ruling and not on the rule's default.**
- **(i) A quotable man gets talked about, and that is the reason for the zero.** `B39-L21-KNOWN-99` registers recognition as his **threat condition**, managed by being *more helpful, less visible, never interesting*; `B39-L33-MISREAD-99` registers that the whole cover depends on being read as **kind and unremarkable**, and a polished maxim is structurally incapable of being unremarkable. `B39-L11-MECH-99` registers his humor as **self-directed understatement**, which is the opposite figure from a balanced maxim. **This was offered as material; the author has now adopted the direction it argued for, and it is recorded here as the reason rather than as the authority.**
- **(ii) What the ruling changed, which is the durability of the zero and nothing else.** Rule 29.4.4.3 makes **the guide is none for any speaker whose ledger Voice Register is not marked epigrammatic**, and his was not so marked, so the operative value was already zero. **A default of that kind can be lifted by a later pass that does not know why the budget was zero. A ruling cannot.** **THE ZERO NOW SURVIVES THE CONDITION THE DEFAULT WAS HANGING ON:** should the cover end, should he cease to be the helpful unremarkable man nobody looks at twice, **the budget does not reopen**, because the author ruled the man and not the disguise.
- **⚠️ BINDING ON EVERY DRAFTING AGENT: A RULED ZERO IS STILL ZERO, AND IS NOW HARDER TO MOVE THAN BEFORE.** **The operative epigram budget for Hercules is ZERO PER SCENE.** Not the default cap of one, and not an open question a drafting agent may resolve in the direction of a good line. **An epigram in a Hercules line is reported at Check #19 as information; the author's zero stands as the register**, and only a further explicit author ruling may change this field.
- **THE FOUR TRAPS, NAMED SO THEY ARE NOT WALKED INTO.** `[DERIVED]`
  1. **Element 1(a)'s repeated sentence is where an epigram will be manufactured, and this is the most important fence in the element.** A sentence built to be delivered twice invites polish. **It must be plain, physical and unbalanced.** It is devastating because he will not move, not because it is finished. **A balanced antithesis in the repeated line breaks this element and mis-writes element 1 in the same sentence**, and the fix is to strip the line back to plain.
  2. **`B39-L08-REFLEX-99`'s registered wording is a ledger sentence and not a line he says.** *He does not argue with people; he stands somewhere, and the standing is the argument* is Section 11 describing him. **It must never be put in his mouth, and it must never arrive as a narrator's summary offered as his motto.**
  3. **Element 1(b)'s flat question must not harden into a catchphrase.** *Has anyone checked* is a **class of question**, not a fixed phrase; the wording varies and the content does not. A fixed repeated formula would be a portable line by another name, and it would additionally collide with element 1(a), whose verbatim repetition only reads as marked because nothing else he says repeats.
  4. **`B39-L11-CONCEAL-99`'s pre-built deflection is not a running joke.** The wry lost-newcomer humor is registered as **the pre-built answer to questions that come too close** — Layer L11 conduct at Check #20, self-directed understatement in content. **Its wording must vary**, and it may never appear as a tag line he produces every time somebody asks where he is from.
- **What is not an epigram and must not be counted as one.** `[DERIVED]` `B39-L18-FORM-99` records that **whatever he once swore by was left with the titles**, so **no inherited oath-form exists to be counted**; `B39-L10-OATH-99` registers that he swears almost never and that the form is plain. A plain oath is not a maxim, and there is no borrowed formal register anywhere in his file to arrive as one.

**6. Hedging versus bluntness (menu item 5) — `[DERIVED]`. THE FRAME DEFERS; THE SENTENCE DOES NOT.**
**Reasoned from the locked material and flagged for author confirmation at the next interview. This is a derivation and is not presented as settled.** *(Ruled `[KEEP PROFILE]` at Check #21 on 2026-10-07; see the ruling bullet below.)*
- **The value, stated precisely.** **He performs deference in the frame and never in the proposition.** The forms of address, the yielded turn, the small self and the physically reduced entry carry the whole of the deference the cover requires. **The sentence inside that frame carries no qualifier at all.** And there is no assertion either: he does not intensify. He states the plain physical fact flat, or he says nothing.
- **The reasoning, laid out so the author can check it.** `[DERIVED]`
  1. **`B39-L13-UP-99` is the load-bearing evidence, and it already contains the split.** The Signature registers that **the cover requires deference and he performs it exactly — while measuring every powerful person against a standard he has never named.** **The performance is a manner; the measure never becomes a word.** `B39-L19-RANKMOD-99` states the same thing from the other side: *rank modulates the performance, not the man.* **A register in which the deference lived inside the sentences would put the measuring on the outside, and the Signature expressly keeps it inside.**
  2. **Element 1(a) makes an unhedged sentence structurally necessary.** A qualified sentence cannot be repeated verbatim as a refusal, because **a qualifier gives the contradiction something to grip.** *Perhaps* invites a counter-perhaps. The repeated identical sentence works precisely because it contains nothing to negotiate. **The two elements are one mechanism.**
  3. **Every registered instance of him declining to commit is a withholding rather than a qualification.** `B39-L08-GAP-99` (he states almost nothing), `B39-L16-CORRECT-99` (corrects by re-demonstration; **the error is never named**), `B39-L15-ANNOUNCE-99` (the announcement is withheld and the feet say it), `B39-L12-CLOSE-99` (the closing word is withheld and the work says it), `B39-L28-AMBIG-99` (an unreadable outcome is filed as not-yet-understood and watched; **he does not force readings**). **Five registrations, one pattern: the assertion is removed, never softened.**
  4. **A man who thinks in loads and methods has nothing to hedge.** `B39-L07-MODE-99` (weights, who bears them, how the thing is done) and `B39-L07-JUDGE-99` (judged by loads carried or dropped, never by words). **A beam either holds or it does not, and a qualifier adds nothing to that sentence.**
  5. **Where he does commit, he commits without cushion.** `B39-L18-WEIGHT-99` (sworn to a face it is unbreakable and kept with frightening literalness), `B39-L12-CONCEDE-99` (concedes terms lightly and persons never), `B39-L12-WEAK-99` (**negotiates from below with hands that could end the table, and neither half shows** — the cleanest evidence in the file that the sentence is flat while the frame is small).
- **The countable form.** **The words *perhaps*, *maybe*, *possibly*, *it may be*, *I think*, *I could be wrong* and *if I am not mistaken* do not appear in a Hercules line.** **Nor do the intensifiers** — *certainly*, *absolutely*, *without question* — because emphasis in this register is the repetition of element 1(a), and an intensifier is a second and different sentence, which element 1(a) forbids. What appears instead is one of exactly three things: **a flat statement of the physical fact**, **a procedural question** (element 2, FREE class), or **nothing**.
- **The apparent exception, and why it is not one. Countable and worth stating.** `[DERIVED]` **The performed deference of `B39-L13-UP-99` and `B39-L19-RANKMOD-99` looks like hedging from outside and is not.** It is address, posture and yielding: **Part 29 Area 16's Address Register Matrix** and Part 39 Layers L13 and L19 at Check #20. **A drafting agent who converts the deference into qualified sentences has moved it from the frame into the proposition and broken this element.**
- **A second apparent exception.** `[DERIVED]` `B39-L11-MECH-99`'s **self-directed understatement** — the enormous man wryly wrong about small local things — understates **himself**, which is content he believes and content the cover requires. **It is a statement, not a qualifier**, and it sets no hedging value.
- **A third apparent exception, and it is the closest thing in the file to a genuine hedge.** `[DERIVED]` `B39-L28-AMBIG-99` (does not force readings) and `B39-L06-SPEED-99` (decides slowly and finally) both look like tentativeness. **Neither reaches the sentence.** The unreadable outcome is filed and watched **without being spoken**, and slowness is delay **before** speech rather than qualification **within** it. When the sentence finally arrives it arrives unqualified, or it does not arrive.
- **Where the flatness acquires heat, and it must not be written as hedging or as argument.** `[DERIVED]` `B39-L03-UNFAKE-99` (he cannot perform indifference to an accusation landing in his hearing) and `B39-L26-INSULT-99` (tolerance bottomless except for his honesty; the room feels the temperature change). **The register does not change shape there. It changes temperature.** The sentences stay short, plain and unqualified; what alters is that he speaks at all, and that element 1(b) fires.
- **Where a genuine soft edge exists, and what it softens.** `[DERIVED]` With children and the young (`B39-L23-CHILD-99`, `B39-L16-PERMIT-99`, `B39-L16-PHYS-99`, `B39-L01-TOUCH-99`). **That is warmth of manner, and the content is not softened even there.** The distinction is the same one this element draws everywhere else.
- **Author confirmation flagged.** `[DERIVED]` **This value does not bear on Rule 29.4.4.1's two-signature floor**, which is discharged by elements 2 and 4, both author-locked. The author may revise or reject this determinant without endangering the field's completeness.
- **Check #21, 2026-10-07 (first draft of Chapter 002, rolled back).** The finding recorded here at the Gate 2 of the first draft (the Area's spoken lines against the listed hedging words) was rolled back 2026-10-07 with the archived Areas. The flag on this element is closed by the ruling in the next bullet.
- **CHECK #21 RULING, 2026-10-07 — `[KEEP PROFILE]`.** The author kept this element exactly as registered, at the second Area in which Hercules spoke in the first draft of Chapter 002 (archived and rolled back 2026-10-07; the ruling is the author's and stands). The value, the countable form and the apparent exceptions stand unchanged, and the flag on this element is closed. The ruling was not made on a D-3 firing, so Section 12 takes no no-change entry and no drift is recorded.

**7. Technical-register default (menu item 7) — REGISTERED BY AUTHOR RULING. The trigger is the author's and is cast-wide; the only thing derived here is how far the register drops.** `[ASKED]` `[DERIVED]`
**GOVERNING RULE — CITATION ONLY.** Parts A and B below restate **Rule 29.4.4.1a(a) and (b)** (Part 29 Area 4 §4.4), which now governs the cast-wide working-conversation trigger and the per-character drop depth centrally, and **the central rule prevails if the two ever diverge.**
**PART A — THE TRIGGER, AUTHOR-RULED, AND STATED IDENTICALLY IN EVERY LEDGER THAT CARRIES THIS ITEM.** `[ASKED]` The author's selected option, verbatim: ***"Whether it is a working conversation."*** **The technical register is ON when the exchange is work being done, and OFF when it is social — regardless of the subject, the place, or who is listening.** The author's own earlier formulation is kept beside it: ***"It depends on the nature of the conversation."*** **The trigger is a cast-wide rule.** Nothing about Hercules produced it, nothing about him modifies it, and **no later pass may narrow, condition or re-anchor it inside this file.**
**PART B — WHAT VARIES PER CHARACTER, AUTHOR-RULED.** `[ASKED]` The author's selected option, verbatim: ***"The rule is the default; each varies in how far it drops"*** — ***"Everyone switches on the same trigger. What is registered per character is how completely the technical register goes off — some drop all the way to plain speech, some never fully leave it."*** **The registrable per-character fact is therefore the DEPTH OF THE DROP, and that depth is the only thing derived below.**
- **HIS DROP-DEPTH — ALL THE WAY DOWN, AND IT HAPPENS INSIDE THE SAME BREATH: THERE IS NO TAIL ON IT.** `[DERIVED]` ***A PROPOSAL, NOT A LOCK. Flagged for author confirmation at Gate 1 Check #21 and not author-locked until the author rules it.*** **Derived from his own registered material and from nothing else.** Element 4's second trigger condition already registers the timing in the field's own words: ***"The moment the subject leaves the work, the length collapses to the default in the same breath."*** **What that clause registers for length, this determinant registers for lexicon, and the collapse is the same collapse.** Three registrations supply the floor it collapses onto: `B39-L08-GAP-99` (***he states almost nothing***), `B39-L10-SILENCE-99` (silence load-bearing and comfortable, ***the resting state of a man who cannot volunteer***) and `B39-L09-MECH-99` (***he never states a falsehood; he never finishes the truth*** — a short sentence has less to leave unfinished). **The consequence is that no craft word survives the subject leaving the work.** There is no thinning stage, no residue, and no half-technical sentence on the way down.
  - **THE COUNTABLE FORM, for Check #19.** `[DERIVED]` **The count of method words in a Hercules line falls to zero in the sentence after the subject leaves the task, not two sentences later.** A drafting agent who lets craft vocabulary trail into the following social exchange has written a tail this element does not have.
  - **THE NEAR-MISS THAT INVERTS IT IS ALREADY WRITTEN AT ELEMENT 4 AND GOVERNS HERE TOO.** `[DERIVED]` `B39-L09-TELL-99` registers that a question that comes too close gets answered with work: **the body moves toward a task and the subject changes with it.** That produces a task in hand and **does not turn this register on.** The task is the exit rather than the subject, and **the lexical count goes DOWN there exactly as element 4's word count does.**
- **⚠️ THE ON HALF IS BOUNDED BY MATERIAL THAT IS ALREADY AUTHOR-LOCKED, AND NOTHING HERE LOOSENS ANY OF IT.** `[ASKED]` `[DERIVED]` Three bounds hold, all cited and none altered. (i) **The concealment law in the boxed note at the head of this field governs every vocabulary choice on the page.** His speech may never carry realm vocabulary above the local ceiling in any ordinary scene, and **a working conversation is an ordinary scene.** (ii) **Element 4's BINDING clause survives this registration intact**: the long register still opens the length **and nothing else**, still **does not raise the vocabulary ceiling**, still admits no §2 realm vocabulary and no Lexicon B, and **item 7 grants item 1 no lexical licence it did not have.** (iii) `B39-L16-TRANSMIT-99`'s registered rail holds without exception: ***nothing transmissible may surface his origin***, so the valley craft he carries — `B39-L29-WATCH-99` `[SIGNATURE]`, `B39-L06-INFO-99`, `B39-L22-RETURN-99` — reaches his mouth **unprovenanced or not at all.** **What the ON half therefore is: the ordinary method vocabulary of the task in front of him, at or below the local ceiling, with no provenance attached.** Element 3's Lexicon B stays bound to the unwatched hour and is not touched by this determinant in either direction.
- **Fences, so that nothing adjacent is disturbed.** `[DERIVED]` **This element does not touch element 2 (item 4) or element 4 (item 1), which discharge the floor, nor elements 5 and 6 (items 6 and 5)**, and none of the four is a lexical value. **It is not element 3 and does not extend it**: element 3 is the registered contextual register break under Rule 29.4.1.2, bound to the unwatched hour, and **a determinant with a stated trigger is provided for by Rule 29.4.4.1 itself and is not a second register.** **It fills no other slot**: items 2, 3, 8 and 9 remain unregistered by choice below and each keeps its own fence.
- **Part 29 Area 12 and Part 30 Law P30-L8, checked and reported as the brief requires, because this file is where the two bindings meet.** `[DERIVED]` **This registration licenses no cultivation or technology vocabulary of any kind and grants no realm word anywhere**; §2's Permitted External Realm References block is unchanged, its three narrative conditions are unchanged, and the boxed note's formulation stands exactly as written — where the two bindings touch, **the tighter binding governs.** Part 29 Area 12's governance of cultivation and technology diction is untouched. **No Phase 1 Forbidden Entity is named, implied or made reachable by this element** — the Empire of Gods and the Three Moon Sects remain barred from prose, dialogue and interior monologue under **P30-L8**, and the Olympus Suppression Seal may be named in speech and thought only as the P30-L8 exception allows.
- **Check #21, 2026-10-07 (first draft of Chapter 002, rolled back).** The finding recorded here at the Gate 2 of the first draft (the Area's lines against element 7) was rolled back 2026-10-07 with the archived Areas; the drop-depth proposal stays flagged and `[DERIVED]`.
- **Check #21, 2026-10-07 (first draft of Chapter 002).** Deferred: the author ruled `[DEFER]` (deferral 1, Section 12). That Deferred row is marked `rolled back 2026-10-07` with the archived Areas (Part 39 Rule 39.41.6.1), so the item stays flagged and unruled; the Area's own finding recorded here was rolled back.

The Chapter touched element 7 and did not exercise it: none of his spoken lines carries a craft word above the local ceiling ("That wants a second cord," "I will take the heavy end," "Has anyone looked at the axle?"), so there was no craft word to drop. The drop-depth proposal stays flagged, `[DERIVED]` and unruled.
- **Check #21, 2026-10-17 (Chapter 002, Ref: 1.1.1.002, text revised in place).** The revised text keeps those three lines word for word and adds no craft word to any line of his, so element 7 was again not exercised and the enquiry read SILENT for him (D-1 to D-4: none found; no unmapped trigger; no profile line moves). The drop-depth proposal stays flagged, `[DERIVED]` and unruled, and Deferred row 2 of Section 12 stands as written.

The author ruled `[DEFER]` again on 2026-10-22 (Deferred row 4, deferral 3, Section 12; deferral 2 was Chapter 006, Deferred row 3). The drop-depth proposal stays flagged, `[DERIVED]` and unruled.

**Menu items 2, 3, 8 and 9 — DELIBERATELY LEFT UNREGISTERED.** `[ASKED]` `[DERIVED]`
Recorded as **unregistered by choice, not as omissions.** The floor of two is discharged by elements 2 and 4. **No default is implied, no value may be inferred from his §11 register, and no later pass may fill any of these slots without an author ruling.** Each is fenced against the §11 or in-field material that sits nearest it:
- **Item 2, fragment tolerance — not registered.** **Not filled by element 4's short default**, since a short sentence is a complete sentence; **not filled by `B39-L10-SILENCE-99` or `B39-L10-FILLER-99`**, which register silence and a placeholder made of motion rather than sound — **absences of speech, Layer L10 conduct at Check #20, and not incomplete sentences.**
- **Item 3, subordination depth — not registered.** **Not filled by element 4's long register**, which registers length and expressly not clause architecture, and **not filled by element 6**, which registers the absence of qualifiers and says nothing about nesting.
- **Item 8, pause and trail-off profile — not registered.** **Not filled by `B39-L10-TURN-99`, `B39-L10-SILENCE-99` or `B39-L10-FILLER-99`** (Layer L10 conduct at Check #20), and **not filled by element 1(a)'s closing silence**, which is the end of his participation in the exchange rather than an unfinished sentence. **His sentences finish**, and a drafting agent must not convert his registered act-filled silences into intra-sentence hesitation.
- **Item 9, figures-first versus conclusion-first ordering — not registered.** **Not filled by element 4's long register.** The order in which he describes a task is the order in which the work is done, which is neither an ordering of image against verdict nor of number against meaning. **No value is set.**

**Registration notes (Part 29 Area 4 compliance).**

- **Rule 29.4.4.4 — Friction Floor source, and the one place this register needs care.** Hercules's per-character friction material draws from his **Part 39 Layer L10** and **Layer L29**, per the §4.4 boundary paragraph. **The difficulty is stated plainly: three of the commonest Friction Floor beats are unavailable from him.** Element 1(a) removes self-correction and rephrasing from inside his sentences; element 6 removes hedging and trailing qualification; element 4's default removes the length a trail-off needs. **It is not thereby exempted.** The floor is satisfied from the registers that are available, and they are unusually rich: **`B39-L09-TELL-99` is the best source in the file** — *a question that comes too close gets answered with work* is **an answer that answers the wrong question**, which Rule 29.4.4.4 names explicitly, and it is sourced, characterological and the opposite of a stumble inserted to pass; `B39-L36-TEXTURE-99` (**the one-demonstration lag — at anything new he is half a beat behind the room**, and the pause before his feet set) supplies the mistimed beat; `B39-L10-INTERRUPT-99` (his body interrupts before his mouth does) supplies the interruption beat with his side of it registered; `B39-L10-FILLER-99` and `B39-L29-HANDS-99` supply **the plain unpolished beat arriving as work rather than as a sentence**; `B39-L10-TURN-99` (yields turns; speaks last, if at all), `B39-L10-APOL-99` (the repair arrives before the words), `B39-L11-RECV-99` (takes being laughed at easily — laughter at his size and his errors), `B39-L01-STARTLE-99` (a human-sized startle half a beat late), and under pressure `B39-L27-RAGE-99` and `B39-L09-CAUGHT-99`. **The Friction Floor is satisfied from those registers; the two prohibitions below are absolute against it.**
- **Rules 29.2.1.1b and 29.2.1.1c — untouched and binding.** The **em-dash is prohibited in the prose body**, and **contractions are categorically prohibited.** *[Canon Amendment 2026-10-08: contractions are now lawful in this character's dialogue (Part 29 Rule 29.2.1.1c as amended) and stay barred in narration; the em-dash prohibition is unchanged, so a later agent's example line obeys the em-dash ban and may use contractions; the traits registered here are delivered in natural, flowing speech and are not sentence formulas (Rule 29.2.1.7).]* No element above is ever rendered with an em-dash or an elision: element 4's short default is complete grammar, element 3's opened length is complete grammar at greater length, element 1(a)'s repeated sentence is a finished sentence delivered twice, and element 1(b)'s question is one finished interrogative. **Sample lines of speech are allowed in this register**; the traits registered above define the speech and the voice, and the few quoted fragments at elements 1(b) and 2 are **permitted-content specimens**. **Any example a later agent writes must obey both prohibitions.**
- **The concealment law and the P30-L8 rail, restated as binding on this field.** §2's **Permitted External Realm References** block is Locked Canon and governs every vocabulary choice on the page; the boxed note at the head of this field states where it and the P30-L8 rail meet, and element 3's rail guard states the practical consequence for the one room where the permission is live. **Canon decisions 1 and 7 are P30-L8-fenced and nothing in this register supplies a verbal route to them**: the proceeding, the false charges, the failure to protect and the father have no permitted referent under the referent test, no question class that could reach them (the NEVER class covers his own account and `B39-L19-CRIT-99` registers that criticism of the divine reaches him in a code he can never answer — he goes still and picks up work, and nothing here changes it), and no attribution device of any kind. **Nothing in this register supplies a drafting agent with a route around either rail.**
- **Item B — closed, used and not reopened.** The **Olympus Suppression Seal** is the operative personal cap and this field uses that vocabulary; the **Firmament Lock** is the outer system ceiling and is not what he presses on. **Neither is reopened, restated at length, or reasoned about here** — `sol-star-system.md` §2 owns the Layered Suppression Model. **The Seal is named in this field as ledger vocabulary; in prose it may be named in speech and thought under the P30-L8 exception.**
- **Rule 39.2.3.1 / the Part 29 boundary paragraph in §4.4.** These two fields are the **Part 29 side only.** Every dimension code above is **cited as an anchor** and its conduct text is untouched. Turn-taking, silence, listening, interruption, refusal form, apology form, praise and criticism delivery, naming habit and oath conduct are **Layer L10** and are cited above as conduct rather than re-registered in prosodic language. **Conduct is audited at Check #20, prosody here at Check #19, and neither substitutes for the other.**
- **Rule 29.4.4.2 — cross-character distinctness.** Per the author ruling, prosodic overlap between characters is permitted and **no distinctness check is run at derivation time.** Per the standing author ruling, **no cross-character comparison appears anywhere in this register, and none may be added to it.** **Bison is named at element 3 because the author-locked material requires the naming; no conduct is registered for him, no dimension or delta is created in his file, no cell is added to anybody's Layer L14 matrix, and no comparison, complementarity or contrast with his register or with any other is registered or implied.** The overlap recorded at element 4 names nobody. §11's own cross-character line stands unchanged: each character is registered separately, overlaps are permitted, and any distinction the story needs is the author's to decide case by case.
- **Rule 39.2.3.2 — no psychological or cultivation value is set here.** No PIS level is set, implied or revised by this field. `B39-L27-PIS5-99` and `B39-L27-PIS7-99` register states and not registers; **no prosodic value is derived from either**, and PIS-driven alteration of speech at draft time is Part 29 Area 10's, applied from the registered state rather than pre-set here. `B39-L31-REVERT-99` is cited and not extended: under a charge-adjacent trigger the register goes to its floor — the short default, no question of any class, and the exit (`B39-L01-EXIT-99`, `B39-L09-CAUGHT-99`) — which is that dimension expressed in speech and is not a new value.
- **Drafting status.** **Hercules now carries a registered Part 29 speech register and prose may be drafted for him as a speaking character.** A scene pairing him with a character whose own register is still unwritten is blocked by **that** character's file, not by his. **The one open canon item — the missing old vocabulary at element 3 — constrains a single rendering choice inside one registered context and does not block his appearance.**

> **NARRATIVE NOTE — MAIN PROTAGONIST & POWER SUPPRESSION:** Hercules is a MAIN primary protagonist. He is a true Demigod — Demigod Level 1 Mid by actual cultivation — but his power is suppressed by the **Olympus Suppression Seal** — a relic-based binding imposed upon him at the betrayal of Mount Olympus — to an effective cap of **Champion Level 8 High**. This creates a profound narrative dynamic: a being of divine-level power trapped within a mortal-tier expression. His actual cultivation is in the Xiantian Realm (Demigod); his effective expression within the Sol Star System is Mortal Tier 2 Champion Level 8 High. He was banished from Olympus on false charges and is currently lost, with no factional affiliation.

## Section 2 — Classification (B/L)

**Cultivation Realm — ACTUAL (Part 3):** Xiantian Realm — Demigod Level 1 Mid
> *Demigod is the first of four Xiantian realms: Demigod → Void God → True God → Absolute God*
> *Level 1 Mid = mid-tier of the first Demigod level; genuine Xiantian being; biological limits broken*
> *This is his real cultivation level achieved prior to entering the Sol Star System*

**Cultivation Realm — EFFECTIVE IN SOL STAR SYSTEM (Part 3 + Olympus Suppression Seal):** Mortal Tier 2 — Champion Level 8 High
> *Champion is the third of five Mortal Tier 2 realms: Senior → Warrior → Champion → Ruler → Monarch*
> *Level 8 High = upper tier of Champion Level 8; near the peak of the Champion realm*
> *The Olympus Suppression Seal caps his effective power output at Champion Level 8 High; he cannot express Demigod-level capabilities at his current phase of suppression*
> *Mechanism: The Olympus Suppression Seal does not reduce his actual cultivation — his Core, meridians, and qi density remain at Demigod level internally. However, his output, expression, and effective capability are suppressed to Champion Level 8 High by the Seal's binding*
> **Phase 1 End-State Target (Part 30, Section 1.3.1 — Locked Canon):** see Section 9 (PHASE 1 END-STATE POWER LEVEL block)

**CC-Class (Part 16):** CC-Class Stratum — effective Champion realm (Mortal Tier 2 mid); true CC-Class (Xiantian Demigod) is suppressed within the Sol Star System
**Cultivation Specialization Types (Part 16 Area 2):** Not yet established — to be defined in prose; Hercules as son of Zeus may have unique divine-heritage cultivation specializations
**Technology Tier (Part 8):** Not yet formally established — to be defined in prose; 50% TA suggests moderate technology integration for a being of his power background
**Integration Type (Part 31, if applicable):** Not yet established — to be defined in prose
**Soul Rung (Part 4):** Not yet established — to be defined in prose; Demigod-level soul cultivation expected
**Meridian Root (Part 4):** Void Root — upper tier of the Refined Spectrum; supports Level 8 Sovereignty affinity at foundation comfort; his Demigod (Xiantian) true cultivation level is consistent with this classification; carries latent capacity for future affinity development and Meridian Root tier progression as Hercules's cultivation matures across later Phases (improvable per Part 4; Olympus Suppression Seal breakthroughs may interact with Meridian Root progression in ways to be defined)
**Affinity Type(s) (Part 4):** Sovereignty (ceiling Level 8, open to change later; the current level follows the Part 4 Area 1.4 realm milestones) — the cultivator reigns over the force from above as its master and supreme authority; Hercules possesses one Sovereignty affinity at Phase 1 start:
  1. **Sovereignty — Strength** (the cultivator commands and governs the force of Strength at the highest authority tier; raw physical and sovereign strength as instrument of power; manifests through unarmed combat — Hercules's hands are his primary weapon and affinity channel; Level 9 expressions reserved for later Phases)
  > *Other affinities (including Life, Death, Reincarnation) and their weapon-binding framework reserved for later Phases per formal Canon amendment — organic emergence through Phase-scale arc design. The Strength affinity is Hercules's defining Phase 1 power axis, channeled through unarmed combat in alignment with his son-of-Zeus identity.*

**Fusion Stage (Part 7, if applicable):** Not yet established — to be defined in prose
**Soul Talent (Part 4):** Profound (Soul Talent Grade 4 of 5) — Hercules possesses Profound-grade soul talent; exceptional natural aptitude consistent with his Demigod-level cultivation and Sovereignty — Strength affinity; Profound talent is the foundation of his combat level advantage of +3 minor steps; his talent is expressed at the full Demigod level internally but suppressed in external expression by the Olympus Suppression Seal
**Combat Level Advantage (Part 3/5):** +3 minor steps above current cultivation level (effective) — Hercules can effectively engage cultivators at 3 minor steps above his suppressed effective level (Champion Level 8 High); one minor step is one move along Low, Mid, High, Peak, then the next Level's Low (one row of the Part 3 Area 1.6 Relative Power Table); his true Demigod-level combat is suppressed by the Olympus Suppression Seal, but his talent-based combat advantage operates within the suppressed effective range; talent-based governance fact, not a realm-cap waiver
**Primary Weapon Classification (Part 33):** Bare Hands (unarmed / body-weapon) — Hercules's hands are his weapon; his Sovereignty — Strength affinity is channeled through unarmed combat; his body itself is the instrument of sovereign Strength expression; this is Hercules's canonical and sole Phase 1 weapon class
  > *Additional weapon forms (Spear, Scythe, Halberd) reserved for later Phases per formal Canon amendment — to be introduced alongside later affinity emergences*
**Technology Interest Level:** Somewhat interested — Hercules has moderate technology awareness; his 50% TA reflects balanced engagement; technology is practically useful but not a passion; cultivation and physical combat are his dominant identity axes
**Military Rank (Part 16 Area 4/5, if applicable):** None current — formerly held rank/position within the Empire of Gods; stripped upon banishment; no current rank within Sol Star System
**Sect Position (Part 16 Area 6, if applicable):** None current — formerly affiliated with Olympus; no current sect affiliation; lost and without faction at story start
**Cultivation Affinity % (CA):** 85% — high cultivation affinity; consistent with his Demigod-level actual cultivation
**Technology Affinity % (TA):** 50% — moderate technology affinity; balanced between cultivation and technology relative to other protagonists
**Cultivation Synergy % (CS%) (Part 31 Area 21):** 70% — reflects Hercules's actual cultivation integration at Demigod level; the Olympus Suppression Seal suppresses his expression but not his internal synergy state
**Technology Synergy % (TS%) (Part 31 Area 21):** 50% — reflects his balanced approach to technology; moderate integration

**OLYMPUS SUPPRESSION SEAL RECORD:**
> **True Realm:** Demigod Level 1 Mid (Xiantian)
> **Effective Cap in Sol Star System:** Champion Level 8 High (Mortal Tier 2)
> **Power Gap:** The gap between Champion Level 8 High and Demigod Level 1 Low spans the rest of the Champion Realm, the whole Ruler and Monarch Realms, and then the Major Realm barrier at Xiantian entry (Demigod Level 1 Low stands at 100 times Unawakened on the Part 3 Area 1.6 Relative Power Table); Hercules's true power dwarfs his effective expression by a factor that makes him among the most powerfully suppressed beings in the Sol Star System
> **Suppression Mechanism:** The Olympus Suppression Seal — a relic-based binding imposed at the betrayal of Mount Olympus — caps Hercules's active expression to Champion Level 8 High. This is distinct from the Firmament Lock (Part 13), which governs the Sol Star System at Core Assimilation Level 3 Mid and does not affect Hercules at his current effective expression level
> **Which suppression governs (Locked Canon):** both apply, layered, and **the tighter binding governs**. The Firmament Lock is the outer, system-wide ceiling and stands over him as it stands over everyone in the system; the Olympus Suppression Seal is the personal inner binding and is the operative cap, which is why he never approaches the Lock's ceiling. Full model — including what neither layer touches and which layer Phase 1 breaks — is owned by `ledger-worlds-location/sol-star-system.md` §2, **Layered Suppression Model**. Not restated here (Part 37 single-source discipline)

**PERMITTED EXTERNAL REALM REFERENCES (Part 3 Rule 1.7 Exception (a) — Locked Canon):**
> Hercules carries verified in-canon exposure to cultivation realms above the Sol Star System's Firmament Lock ceiling through his life on Olympus and within the Empire of Gods prior to banishment. He may canonically reference the following realm vocabulary in prose (thought, speech, memory, identity), and narration of him may use this vocabulary when reflecting his consciousness:
>
> **Permitted (External Exposure):**
> - **Mortal Tier 2 summit:** Ruler (all levels), Monarch (all levels)
> - **Xiantian:** Demigod, Void God, True God, Absolute God (all levels)
> - **Core Realm:** Core Formation, Core Assimilation, Core Solidification (all levels)
> - **Divine Realm:** Divine Transformation, Sea Formation, Heaven Treading, Divine Ascension, Divine Sea Realm (all levels — subject to Part 28 Area 12.2 authorization protocol for narrative deployment of Absolute-tier cultivators)
>
> **Narrative Conditions for Reference:**
> - Internal thought or memory of Olympus (admissible; the word Olympus, the Seal, the reasons for the banishment and the promise to clear his name may be spoken or thought in Phase 1 prose under the P30-L8 exception); the Empire of Gods and every other name for the origin are ledger reference only, barred from Phase 1 prose by P30-L8, interior thought included
> - Self-identity statement (e.g., "I am Demigod Level 1 Mid" — always admissible; part of his MAIN protagonist identity)
> - Dialogue with Bison or other external-exposure characters (admissible)
> - Dialogue with in-system characters: admissible as a narrative reveal under Part 3 Rule 1.7 exception (c) when Hercules explicitly discloses his external origin; otherwise, Hercules conceals this vocabulary from in-system interlocutors and the narration follows his concealment
>
> **Always-Forbidden Terms (All Contexts — Part 28 Area 12.2):**
> Nascent Soul, Foundation Establishment, Qi Condensation (as realm label), Golden Core, Spirit Severing, Dao Seeking, Mahayana, Tribulation Transcendence, Eternal Life, Immortal Ascension. These are non-canonical and never apply — not even to Hercules's external exposure.

*[L — Updated at every breakthrough, classification change, rank advancement, or CS%/TS%-triggering event; effective cap status updated if Olympus Suppression Seal conditions change; Permitted External Realm References is immutable and governed by Part 3 Rule 1.7 exception (a)]*
