**Soul Sense Range (Part 14 Area 4.1, if applicable):** Demigod-level soul sense internally; effective expression within the Sol Star System is suppressed. Shown in prose in Chapter 002 (Ref: 1.1.1.002), text revised in place 2026-10-17, and no more than the page shows: his sense reads a person's breath, warmth and qi, or the lack of it, at close range, one person near him at a time (Lowen Wray at the well; Pasco Cobb, Kitto Hale and Newlyn Pengelly), a whole mill yard in one sweep (Senara Polglase on her bin, Jowan Angove at his rope and the lads at the sacks), a lane (Enyd Carrow) and Bison each time he comes back along the grass with the empty. It finds a shown rank and names it in his mind in the page's own form (***Champion Level 8 High***, ***Senior Level 1 Low***), and it finds absence as plainly as presence. Beneath Bison's shown rank it feels something that pushes, steady and unread, which it can feel and cannot measure. The page turns it on people only and never on the forest. No distance figure is given and none is invented (OPEN-AREA). *Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006), 2026-10-20: the sense is turned on the forest and its beasts for the first time ("He did what he had not done in a year, and sent his sense out into the forest"), so the clause "people only and never on the forest" describes Chapter 002 and no longer the whole page. He lets it out "as thin as the first" of his three sizes and "it came back full"; it reads a Kind as a worded range ("from the middle of the second Level to the high end of the fourth"), every beast of the host as a Warrior, the villagers by their level strings (Selyf Chenoweth, Yestin Hocking, Morwenna Keast and Jory Michell, and Enyd Carrow and Uthno Nancarrow again) and the lanes as "all of them unawakened", and in full dark it finds nothing where a man stands at the forest's edge, "a gap in the reading the size of a man". No distance figure is given and none is invented; see the first-use facts of Chapter 006 below.* **Chapter 008 (Ref: 1.1.1.008), 2026-10-22:** the sense is turned outward "as the guard of the hour, at the rank he showed the world" (the shown rank, ***Champion Level 8 High***) down the track, along the log slide to the mill, over the strips as far as the hedge and back up through the first yards of the trees, and finds "no breath on the track, nothing on the slide, and nobody on the shelf" but Bison, who reads "the same rank as his own, kept small, with the steady push beneath it that his sense could feel and had never learned to measure". No distance figure is given and none is invented; see the first-use facts of Chapter 008 below.
**Known Techniques Outside Named Move System:** Not yet established — to be defined in prose; divine-origin techniques from Olympus training are implied
**First-use facts of power, Chapter 002 (Ref: 1.1.1.002), text revised in place 2026-10-17 (cultivation visibility, Part 29 Rule 29.12.1.3), what the page shows and no more:** In his own mind he keeps the sizes of himself in order (Area block 01): the strong man's share, the only size the village has ever met; the most he can let out, ***Champion Level 8 High***, with its one picture clause (a rank that makes strangers brace as if for a blow); and, beneath that second size, something that pushes, steady and without a name he can give it, which lays the grass flat without a wind, with a weight lying over it that holds it down. In a year neither has eased. The page never names or numbers the third size, never names a maker of the weight and puts the weight nowhere on his body; the Seal is not named. At Kitto Hale's cask (Area block 02) the heat is the thing beneath the Champion, pushing outward, and the weight is what presses back on it; the cask asks only for the strong man's share, and the Champion in him could have set it on the cart with one hand. At the loaded cart (Area block 04) the Champion in him could have ended it with one hand and a stride and he uses none of it, what pushes beneath lies under the weight and the weight would not have let it out, and three leans is all the lane believes a strong man has. In the mill yard he stands at the edge of a crowd of Unawakened people as a second Champion beside Bison's gloved hand (Area block 03). The flicker watch (`B39-L02-THREAT-99`) is shown once, in the lane (Area block 04): at Enyd Carrow, in whom his sense finds a trace of qi so faint that a candle flame at her elbow would not have stirred (***Senior Level 1 Low***), he watches her face for the second look, the hitch of someone who has felt more in him than a strong back, and it does not come. His sense finds the same faint trace in Uthno Nancarrow at the forest's edge (Area block 05), and no qi in anyone else the Chapter names; nobody practises. The true rank of Section 2 stays in the ledger and is not written in Chapter 002: the author's rulings for the revision of this Chapter keep the Seal unnamed, keep the word Demigod out of the prose and waive, for this Chapter, the clause of Rule 29.12.1.3 that asks for both levels of a sealed character. No cell of Section 2 or of this Section is changed by these facts.

**First-use facts of power, Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006), Gate 2 of 2026-10-20, what the page shows and no more:** At dusk on 1247.2.15 / PD 43, at the head of the log slide, he holds himself "in three sizes" in his own mind (the strong man's share that the village knows; ***Champion Level 8 High***, "a rank that would make a stranger set his feet as for a blow"; and under it a third "which pushed and had no name he could give it, with a weight laid over it that had not shifted in a year"), and for the first time in a year sends his sense into the forest, reading the host's six Kinds as worded ranges (Area block 01). The heat and the weight under the run: "The heat rose in him as the run went on ... and the weight settled over it, flat and even, and between the two there was no room to run any faster than a strong man runs. He let nothing out" (Area block 01); under each save in the lanes the heat climbs and the weight holds, and he lifts the forge door's beam "as high as a strong man's arms can put a load and no higher" (Area block 02). The cover breaks in Area block 03: "The year went out of him with that stride as a latch drops ... He had spent it carrying a little less than he could, so that nobody would count what he carried, and now the count was open." The hooks of a Hook Wing "took hold of the bare skin and could not get in" (***Hide Brace***); in the quiet after ***Heavy Palm*** the weight "gave a finger's width and no more, and the ease of it went through him like warm water, and then it closed again, and he did not follow it"; after ***Immovable Stand*** at the bakery door it gives "a little more than before" and closes. In the mill square the second ***Immovable Stand*** leaves the weight over the heat giving "nothing at all", and the one ***Staggering Blow***, "heavier than anything he had let out in a year", takes a buck's footing and guard while "the heat went up under the weight as far as it could go, and the weight held, and held him to the one blow he had chosen and no more"; his chest burns afterward (Area block 04). The flicker watch (`B39-L02-THREAT-99`) is shown twice: in Area block 03 he "looked for the way out of the light" and found the lane full of wolves, and stood with Bison back to back in the torn lane, "a man standing with his friend and not a man looking for a way out"; on Day 17 (1247.2.17 / PD 45) he feels the village looking at his back, watches for "the catch in it, the second glance", finds it, "and found that the village had put it in its pocket and was not going to take it out" (Area block 06). At dusk on Day 17 "the old weight lay on him again, with the heat banked under it ... The give he had felt in the yard had closed" (Area block 06). He keeps the host's conduct ("Wolves and bears and foxes, which keep to their own, had run as one. They had not fed. They had not turned back") and the man at the forest's edge as "the fourth fact" beside the other three, "a thing he could name and could not lift", and tells no one (Area block 06). The page shows the current level only: the Seal is not named, the true level is not given and no maker of the weight is named. No cell of Section 2 or of this Section's Baseline lines is changed by these facts, and Evolution Entries 5 to 8 (Section 14) record the author's ruling on his exuberance.

**First-use facts of power, Chapter 008 (Ref: 1.1.1.008), Gate 2 of 2026-10-22, what the page shows and no more:** On the evening of 1247.2.21 / PD 49, on the shelf of high ground at the forest's edge where the timber track beside the log slide comes out (a post of new ash at the head of the slide with a common field bell on it, and a flat stone beside the post), the village's watch has put him and Bison there as a pair, and "a pair on the watch was left alone". Before he sits he turns his sense outward "as the guard of the hour" at the shown rank and finds no one within sight or hearing (see the Soul Sense Range line). Then the day's cover comes off: "The shoulders he had kept a hand narrower since dawn came down, and the breath that had taken a strong man's share all day took what it wanted. The heat lay banked where it was, and the weight rested over the heat as it had for a year; neither eased and neither pushed". He sits on the flat stone and goes still, his hands open on his knees. At Bison's question whether they hold to what they chose, "The weight bore down on the heat, and the stillness came over him from the hands inward" (Area block 01); as he asks Bison "Do you want it said?", "The heat stirred under the weight as he asked, and settled when the asking was done" (Area block 02). The weight is named aloud for the first time: "My father put the Seal on me" is "the first time he had named the weight aloud, the binding that lay over the heat in him". The page does not say the Seal is worn or embodied, and it names no maker beyond "my father" (no name, title, place or polity). The two men take the Seal as a binding that "will take a few years to break" and, until then, "builds character" and grounds a man; that is their belief and it sets no cell of Section 2 or of this Section. In his thought, once, in one sentence and as designations: "Under the rank he showed, ***Champion Level 8 High***, he was ***Demigod Level 1 Mid***, and Bison, under the same shown rank, was ***Demigod Level 1 Mid*** as well", told to himself "in plain words and privately, what he had never said where another could hear" (the first page use of the self-identity permission of Section 2 and of `B39-L07-SELF-99`), followed by "A few years of the Seal stood between that and the day either of them could say it where any man could hear." The page gives no number, step or gap for the two levels. The flicker watch (`B39-L02-THREAT-99`) is at rest in the hour and recalled once: when a drover asked at noon what had burned at the edge of the village and was told it was last winter's thatch, he "watched the room for a second look at either of us, and it did not come". The page shows no Named Move and no fight. No cell of Section 2 or of this Section's Baseline lines is changed by these facts.

### Relational Address Standards (Part 29 Area 16)

*(Part 29 **Area 16 — Relational Address and Respect Law**, subsections numbered 13.1 through 13.14 and cited below as §13.x. This block writes **address TERMS only.** **Naming HABIT is Part 39 Layer L10 and is ALREADY REGISTERED** at `B39-L10-NAME-99` and its neighbours in §11.1; those lines are **cited here and are not duplicated, restated, re-registered, contradicted or extended**. Per **Rule 39.2.3.1** the Section 1 Voice Register and Prosodic Signatures fields are Part 29 Area 4's, are complete, and are **not implied anywhere below.** Per the standing author ruling, **no cross-character comparison of any kind appears**: every form below is derived from this character's own canon and from the Area 16 and kingdom-level sources named, and from nothing else.)*

⚠️ **THE FIRST QUESTION, ANSWERED BEFORE ANY FORM IS CHOSEN: NO ADDRESS SYSTEM IS HIS.** `[DERIVED]`
**He is inside no kingdom's address system by membership, and canon is unambiguous on the point.** §2 records **Military Rank: None current** and **Sect Position: None current**; §7 records **Faction Affiliation: None current — lost and unaffiliated at story start**; §11.5 records **Active collective codes: none** — *"no faction, sect, rank, or registered village Collective Behavior Code binds him; he is the border village's guest, not its member"* — and `B39-L19-CONFORM-01` stands `[N-A]` for exactly that reason. **Rule U-1 says internal hierarchy produces vocabulary; he belongs to no internal hierarchy, so he generates none.** §13.13's matrix has **no row for a person of no faction, no rank and no office**, and that absence is the finding, not an oversight to be filled by invention.

**What governs him instead, and it is never nothing — P29-13.6 is explicit that the universal framework applies to all characters in all factions, with faction modifiers added on top.** Two universal rules carry his whole system:
- **Rule U-2, Host Territory Default, is the operative mechanism.** The host territory's address conventions govern the meeting context. **He is permanently a guest on everyone's ground** (`B39-L19-GUEST-99`), so **the host's conventions govern every exchange he is ever in** — and his registered conduct is already precisely that: `B39-L18-FORM-99` (**plain words, local forms copied exactly**) and `B39-L20-SEAL-99` (**performs local formal acts exactly as watched, one demonstration behind, correctly forever**). **Area 16 and Part 39 describe the same act from two sides. Neither is adjusted here.**
- **Rule U-4, New Encounter Default, supplies his standing level.** The most formal applicable register, with informality descending as relationship is demonstrated. ⚠️ **U-4 fixes the LEVEL and never the WORD** — a point the Azure Sky block states in its own terms — and the descent half collides with his registered conduct; see **THE PERFORMED-DEFERENCE SEAM** below.

⚠️ **WHICH HOST TERRITORY IS NOT SETTLED BY CANON, AND THAT IS THIS BLOCK'S HEADLINE.** `[AUTHOR-PENDING]`
**The border village is unnamed and its jurisdiction is recorded two different ways.** `ledger-worlds-location/azure-sky-kingdom.md` lists it under **Notable Sub-Locations within Azure Sky Kingdom**; §1 of this file, `ledger-worlds-location/_index.md` and `nine-suns-continent.md` describe it as the village **between** or **near** Azure Sky Kingdom **and neighbouring kingdoms not yet named.** **If it is Azure Sky ground, the Azure Sky Relational Address System block governs him and the forms below marked *where the host is Azure Sky* are live. If it is a neighbour's ground, that kingdom is unnamed and holds no address system in canon at all, and he has no address vocabulary whatever.** **This block does not choose.** A second `[AUTHOR-PENDING]` rides on the first: that kingdom's **trade-as-title** ruling is **expressly scoped to that kingdom**, with the generalisation routed as its own open item 13 — so even proximity does not carry it here by itself.

**ANSWERED for the border village, 2026-10-07.** `[ASKED]` The village is Greenhollow Cross, on Azure Sky ground, at the border of the Azure Sky Kingdom (the author, 2026-10-06; the border wording 2026-10-07; `ledger-worlds-location/greenhollow-cross.md`). **The Azure Sky Relational Address System block governs him in the village, and the forms marked *where the host is Azure Sky* are live there.** The other branch (ground between unnamed neighbouring kingdoms, with no address vocabulary at all) is closed for this village. Whether the trade-as-title rule generalises beyond the Azure Sky Kingdom stays that kingdom's own open item 13 and is **not decided here**. The paragraph above is kept as written, as the record of the question.

⚠️ **§13.10 — THE TWO HIERARCHIES, AND THE RULE MATTERS MORE FOR HIM THAN FOR ALMOST ANYONE.** `[DERIVED]`
**§13.2 and §13.10 make social/political rank and cultivation rank independent authorities.** **He is the case where the two diverge to their maximum, and the divergence produces NOTHING on the page.**
- **His social rank is nil and perfectly readable.** Nothing in the social hierarchy attaches to him, so **§13.10's own default row for mixed informal settings governs him permanently: *social register is the default in the Starward Saga world.*** Every ordinary scene of his is a social-hierarchy scene.
- **His cultivation rank is enormous and unreadable.** §2 records **Demigod Level 1 Mid actual / Champion Level 8 High effective**, and §2's concealment default makes the fact unavailable to every in-system interlocutor. **A hierarchy nobody can read cannot govern an address form.** 11.7 registers what people receive instead, and `L24-SENSE-99` registers that **nobody reads him reading them.**
- ⚠️ **Rule P29-13.10.2 is a RAIL for him, not a tool.** The Rule treats an explicit assertion of cultivation authority inside a social context as a **register shift** requiring characterization support. **For him it is not a register shift; it is the concealment law breaking**, and §2's three narrative conditions are the only places a self-identity statement is admissible. **He never invokes it.** A drafting agent who has him claim a cultivation register to win a room has breached §2, which is graver than an Area 16 error.
- **The consequence, plainly:** **he holds the highest cultivation rank in nearly every room he stands in, and it produces zero address consequence in either direction** — nobody reaches him by it, and he never claims it.

| **The village** (11.4a) | **The trade word for his work, with the name after it** — the mechanism reaches him because his work is visible; ⚠️ **the particular word waits on first Area use and MAY NOT BE PICKED HERE** | Where the host is Azure Sky, that block's Establishment Threshold fixes the particular trade word for a particular person at first use, exactly as an office form fixes `[AUTHOR-PENDING]`. **Answered for Chapter 002 (the author, 2026-10-06, accepted as recommended 2026-10-07):** the villagers call him by his bare first name, with no trade word, title or species word, and no trade word is set for him in the Chapter; the trade-word slot stays open (open item 3). **Shown in Chapter 002 (Ref: 1.1.1.002):** Lowen Wray greets him in the opening exchange with the bare name, "Hercules," and the toll collector, Pasco Cobb, at the bridge with "Hercules, that is two buckets for the bakery, so go through." (the bare name, then what he carries and where; the line is the text revised in place on 2026-10-17, which replaced "Hercules. Two buckets, bakery. Go through."); no villager uses a trade word, a title or a species word for him |
| **A sovereign or an office-holder** | **By name where the record carries it, by the matter where it does not** — or **by the trade word where his work is known** | Rule P29-13.8.4 and the host kingdom's CAT-6 instantiation `[DERIVED]` |
| **Bison** | ⚠️ **NOT DECIDED HERE** | Bison's side of the bond is his own file's. **This block registers no conduct and no term for him and pre-decides nothing** |
| **Any cultivation form** | ⚠️ **NONE** | He holds no sect position (§2). **CAT-7's ladder has no slot for him and none may be created** |
| ⚠️ **Reputation labels** | ⚠️ **NOT ADDRESS TERMS** | The outlander labels carried at `B39-L21-KNOWN-99` (S2+) and 11.7 `L33-REP-99` (S4+) are **Part 39 read-by-others reputation facts.** Whether either is ever spoken **to** him is **not established** `[AUTHOR-PENDING]`, and **converting one into a vocative creates an address form from a source Area 16 does not own** |

⚠️ **THE PERFORMED-DEFERENCE SEAM — `B39-L13-UP-99` AND `B39-L19-RANKMOD-99`. THE TERMS FIRST, THEN THE COLLISION.**
**The terms his registered conduct reaches for.** `B39-L13-UP-99` `[ASKED]` `[SIGNATURE]` registers that **the cover requires deference and he performs it exactly — while measuring every powerful person against a standard he has never named**; `B39-L19-RANKMOD-99` states it from the other side — **rank modulates the performance, not the man.** In Area 16 terms that resolves to a single instruction and it governs every upward row above: **he reaches for the most formal applicable form the host territory supplies, every time, and he performs it exactly as watched** (`B39-L18-FORM-99`, `B39-L20-SEAL-99`). **The measure never becomes a word** — it is registered as inward and unnamed, and **no address form carries it.** **The performance is the manner and the term; the measuring stays inside.** **Both lines are cited and neither is adjusted.**

⚠️ **THE COLLISION, REPORTED AND DELIBERATELY NOT RESOLVED.** `[AUTHOR-PENDING]` **Rule U-4's second half says informality DESCENDS with demonstrated relationship. Canon pulls both ways on whether his does.** **Toward descent:** `B39-L13-TRUST-99` (**persons only — instant and total for a face that has shown one demonstrated decency**) and 11.4a's village entry (**the cover grown warm, and he has not noticed the seam move**). **Against descent:** `B39-L19-RANKMOD-99`'s framing of the deference as a *performance* keyed to rank, and `B39-L13-MASK-99` (**the private difference is spent in one hour a day, and nowhere else**). **Neither side is adjusted, narrowed or reconciled by this block, and neither is chosen. An author ruling is required.** Until it is made, **the standing form is the exact performed level**, because that is the half the registered conduct states in its own words.

⚠️ **THREE RAILS BIND EVERY TERM ABOVE. THEY ARE CITED, NOT CREATED.**
1. **Part 30 Law P30-L8.** The **Phase 1 Forbidden Entities** may not appear in Phase 1 prose, dialogue or interior monologue **in any form.** **They are not named in this block, and they may not be named in prose even in order to state the prohibition — cite the Law instead.** **No address term may derive from, gesture at, or require one of them.** §11's header carries the rail and is cited, not restated.
2. ⚠️ **THE OLD VOCABULARY IS CLOSED TO ADDRESS, ABSOLUTELY.** `[IMMUTABLE]` His origin language **has no established name** and **canon establishes no word of it** — recorded as open canon in this file, with `B39-L18-FORM-99` (**whatever he once swore by was left with the titles**), `B39-L05-RITUAL-99` and `B39-L16-TRANSMIT-99` all recording that nothing of that origin survives on the page. **The registered permit list is CLOSED and its prohibition list is EXHAUSTIVE, and a title is on the prohibition list.** **An address term is a title. Therefore no address term may come from there — not in company, not alone, and not inside the one registered unwatched hour.** **The permit list is not extended by this block, no word may be coined, and the language may not be named.** If a scene appears to need one, **stop and flag it** (Part 28 Area 20 Entity Naming Law; Gate 1 Check-17).
3. **NO ADDRESS TERM MAY CARRY REALM VOCABULARY.** §13.10 decides which **hierarchy** is active; **it never decides which words are legal.** §2's **Permitted External Realm References** and its three narrative conditions govern that, unchanged, and §2's default stands: **he conceals the vocabulary from in-system interlocutors and the narration follows his concealment.** **Nothing in Area 16 grants a realm word anywhere, and this block grants none.**

**THE THREE MISTAKES MOST LIKELY WHEN DRAFTING HIM.** *(§13.14 error classes; severities as that section grades them.)*
1. ⚠️ **GIVING HIM A WORD WHERE CANON SUPPLIES NONE — the likeliest of all, because the gap stays invisible until a stranger speaks.** **U-4 fixes the level and never the word**; §13.5.3's deferential forms are the CAT-3 child-to-peer-child pre-introduction default and **are not extended to him**; and where the host is Azure Sky, a person of no office with no known trade and no function in the matter has **no term at all**, by that block's own open item. **A courtesy word reached for here is a coinage.** **MAJOR.**
2. ⚠️ **CONVERTING A REPUTATION LABEL INTO A VOCATIVE.** The outlander labels are **Layer L21 and Layer L33 read-by-others facts, not Area 16 terms.** Speaking one at him invents an address form from a source Area 16 does not own. **MAJOR.**
3. ⚠️ **LETTING A CULTIVATION-HIERARCHY FORM TOUCH HIM IN EITHER DIRECTION.** A **Sect Master**, **Elder**, **Master**, **Senior** or **Junior** form reaching him is a **CAT-7 sect form and he belongs to no sect** (§2) — a §13.14 **MINOR** at best and a mapping error at worst; and **a cultivation register claimed by him breaks §2's concealment law**, which is graver than any Area 16 grade.

**OPEN ITEMS — FLAG, DO NOT INVENT.**

> - Row 2 The Olympus Suppression Seal: planted in Section 1, paid in Sections 5 to 7, with the one Section 8 Catastrophic-type use that the Seal punishes. Status: planted (Ref: 1.1.1.002), felt as daily weather and never named: the late cold on the first morning, then the heat and the weight over it at Kitto Hale's full cask on Day 1 and again at the loaded cart on Day 2, each held to what a strong man could give; returning in Chapters 006 and 008. In the text revised in place on 2026-10-17 the weight is shown against the sizes of himself (his shown rank, ***Champion Level 8 High***, and an unnamed push beneath it that the weight holds down), still with no name for the Seal, no maker and no place on his body (Section 3). Carried in Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006): the heat and the weight are shown again under a run, a rescue and a fight, and under a joy (the weight gives a finger's width in the bakery yard and closes, and by dusk on Day 17 the old weight lies on him again), still unnamed; returning in Chapter 008. Carried in Chapter 008 (Ref: 1.1.1.008): named on a page for the first time, aloud, in the unwatched hour ("My father put the Seal on me"), the weight identified as "the binding that lay over the heat in him"; placed on him by his father (unnamed), and taken by the two men as a binding that "will take a few years to break" and "builds character" (their belief, not a ledger fact); his true level set once, in his thought, under the shown ***Champion Level 8 High***. Not paid; payment stays in Sections 5 to 7.
> - Row 3 A cause of their own: planted in Sections 1 to 3, paid in Sections 7 and 8. Status: planted lightly (Ref: 1.1.1.002): in the lane he puts himself between the loaded cart and the child at the ditch, stands beside the blamed lad and puts one flat question to the whole lane; returning in Chapters 006 and 008. Carried in Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006), beginning to form lightly: in the hall he and Bison say "This is our village, and we mean to stay in it a while" and ask that the work stay as it was (Area block 05), and the village plans its watch with the two beside it (Area block 06); no flat question is put in this Chapter; returning in Chapter 008. Carried in Chapter 008 (Ref: 1.1.1.008), taking shape: the promise made on the road out (to step away from what they had been, to change who they were, to find out who they were and, in time, to find the proof that they were never guilty) is kept by conduct with no oath ("No oath was said on that road, and none has been said since, because the year is how we keep it"), and the proof is "a load ... carried to someone" who does not already believe them; they are "not carrying it yet". No flat question is put in this Chapter.

**Open Questions / Scene-Seeds (Part 28 Area 17):**
- What goes unsaid between the two men: at the well Bison draws a breath that goes out into the wind with no word on it (Chapter 002, Ref: 1.1.1.002), and at the end of the crossing he looks across the bridge at Hercules for some time while nothing is said by either. The page names neither what he was about to say nor what the look held. Carried to Chapters 006 and 008. Chapter 008 (Ref: 1.1.1.008): the fall is spoken between the two in outline in the unwatched hour, but what Bison was about to say at the well and what the look across the bridge held are still not named. OPEN-AREA
- The watching at the field's edge: twice on Day 3 he feels watched at the outer edge of his strip and finds only the hedge, the ditch, the trunks and a sow with her snout in the leaves. The page gives no watcher. OPEN-AREA
- The lane after the cart: Enyd Carrow says the hall will want to hear that a child stood at the ditch and a loaded cart ran loose in the lane; whether the hall hears, and what the carter, Jowan Angove, does about his lad, Neot Hosken, once the axle is found worn, are not shown. The cart stands in the wheelwright's yard on two props with its near wheel off on Day 3. OPEN-AREA
- How the village reads him next: the lane looked at him a beat longer than it needed to after he stopped the cart and put the one question (Day 2), and the carriers looked back at him kindly and went forward again when he looked up (Day 3). What the village makes of the man who stopped a loaded cart with his shoulder is not shown. OPEN-AREA
- The two faint traces: in the text revised on 2026-10-17 his sense finds a trace of qi so faint that a candle flame would not have stirred in Enyd Carrow, the basket-weaver, in the lane (Day 2) and the same faint first rank in Uthno Nancarrow, the forester, at the forest's edge (Day 3), each written as ***Senior Level 1 Low***, and no qi in the other ten villagers the Chapter names. Nobody practises on the page. The page does not say whether either of the two knows what they carry, where it came from or whether they will ever practise, and no one reads him back (Enyd's second look does not come). OPEN-AREA
- What the village does with what it saw (Chapter 006, Ref: 1.1.1.006): the village watched him take a Hook Wing's hooks across his bare back and put a wolf through a fence, and the Chief ruled that what was seen stays in the village; on Day 17 he looks in the village's look at his back for the second glance, finds it, and finds that the village has "put it in its pocket and was not going to take it out". Whether the second glance ever comes out, and what the village asks of the two, are not shown. OPEN-AREA
- The silhouette (Chapter 006): a man stood at the forest's edge in full dark with his hands in his sleeves, and Hercules's sense found nothing in him; he told no one and holds it as "the fourth fact" beside the host's strange conduct. Who the man is, and whether he comes again, are not shown to any character. Chapter 008 (Ref: 1.1.1.008): Hercules tells Bison the silhouette ("a man stood motionless against the pale lines of the trunks in a coat to his knees, with his hands in his sleeves, and I saw no face"; "I set the man beside those three as a fourth, and I told no one"), so it is no longer held by him alone; the man is still unknown to both. OPEN-AREA
- The host's conduct (Chapter 006): twenty-eight beasts of six Kinds ran as one, did not feed and did not turn back; the page gives no cause to any character. Chapter 008 (Ref: 1.1.1.008): the two set it beside the file at the far ford and read it as sent ("Someone sent them"; Bison: "Men sent this"), with no name, no kingdom, no reason and no will in the beasts ("I have no name for them, and no reason, and I will not make either one"); who sent them, why and whether they will send again stay unknown to both. OPEN-AREA
- The shared unspoken: on Day 17 Bison stands "a long stride off" beside him at the hedge and neither speaks, each holding a sight the other does not know (Hercules the silhouette, Bison the file at the far ford). Carried to Chapter 008. **Paid at Chapter 008 (Ref: 1.1.1.008), closed:** in the unwatched hour each tells the other what he held for six days, Bison the file of about two dozen men in plain coats crossing a ford by shaded lanterns and the dead lying with their heads to the roofs and nothing fed on, Hercules the silhouette and the host's three departures, and neither blames the other ("I kept mine as long as you kept yours"; "There was nothing in it to blame").
- The fathers and the uncle (Chapter 008, Ref: 1.1.1.008): Hercules suspects, with no proof, that "my uncle" made the false proof and that "my father" had a use for the Seal and never told him what it was, and says of both men's fathers that they "used it, or let it stand, because it served what they wanted"; Bison says the same of his own father. Neither suspicion is proven or named; who the uncle is, what the fathers wanted and why the exile was "necessary to what they wanted" are not shown. OPEN-AREA
- The proof (Chapter 008, Ref: 1.1.1.008): the two are "not carrying it yet"; he does not know "to whom, or what it weighs, or where it is kept", only that it will have to be set down "in front of someone who does not already believe us". OPEN-AREA
- The village's hold (Chapter 008, Ref: 1.1.1.008): he says aloud, to Bison only, that "it costs more each month that the village grows on us", that the village put a bell on a post and a pair of them beside it "without being asked", that he does not yet know where to set that down, and that "The day we leave it, if we leave it, will cost the village too". What he will do about it is not shown. OPEN-AREA

**Narrative summary (as of Chapter 002, Ref: 1.1.1.002).** Hercules is met at first light on the first morning of spring (1247.1.1 / PD 1) at the Hedge Well at the top of the slope of Greenhollow Cross, a village at the edge of Skyvein Forest. The rope comes up furred white. He draws it hand over hand, six pulls and a pause before the seventh, and carries each full bucket at the pace of a man who means to spill nothing; his eyes go to the grass before they go to the water, and Bison is there on the open side. The two work in step and talk as they work: the rope is going at the hook, and the yard starts on the seed tomorrow and will want every back it can get. In his own mind he keeps three sizes of himself in order, the strong man's share, the most he can let out (***Champion Level 8 High***) and, beneath that, something unnamed that pushes under a weight; his sense finds Bison at the same shown rank with the same unread push beneath it, and finds no qi in Lowen Wray. A time after Lowen Wray pulls her collar higher he finds his own hanging loose at his throat and turns it up. Lowen greets the two by their bare first names and tells him that Kitto Hale needs someone for the heavy end of a full cask in the inn yard and that she named him; his voice slows into the cover, a pause before every word and the smile after the words, and he sets off down the path under the yoke. At the crossing he goes slowly through the waking village like a man wading among children, looking at what each person carries and saying so once ("That wants a second cord"), takes Lowen's joke about the loaves that go to him ("He counts short, because it is one loaf in four."), and at Kitto Hale's cask the heat comes up from no place he can point to and a flat weight settles over it, pressing down, and between heat and weight there is no room left to move in; he goes dead still for one breath, then lifts the cask as a strong man would, and the weight eases by a small measure. The heat is the thing beneath the Champion, pushing outward, and the weight is what presses back on it; the cask asks only for the strong man's share. Across the bridge Bison has turned out of the wind and is looking at him; nothing is said. On the second day (1247.1.2 / PD 2), at the mill yard, he takes two sacks where the lads take one, watches the carter Jowan Angove lash a load once, ties the same knot with the carter's third wrap and earns the carter's "Hm". His sense goes once over the yard and comes away empty, Unawakened to the last lad, and he stands at the edge of the crowd round Bison's gloved hand as a second Champion. In the lane the same day a loaded seed cart slides loose with its brake hard down and a child, Ebrel Tangye, at the lip of the ditch; he drops his sack, meets the cart with his shoulder and gives it three careful steps of strength, no more than a strong man could give, until Bison's wedge stops it. The Champion in him could have ended it with one hand and a stride and he uses none of it; what pushes beneath lies under the weight, and the weight would not have let it out. In the lane his sense finds a trace of qi in Enyd Carrow, the basket-weaver (***Senior Level 1 Low***), and he watches her face for the second look, which does not come. He hands the child to the woman who runs for her, stands beside the blamed lad in the line of the carter's pointing arm, and puts one flat question to the whole lane ("Has anyone looked at the axle?"), then waits without moving until the wheelwright, Newlyn Pengelly, finds the axle end worn through at the neck and says it is no lad's doing. The lane looks at him a beat longer than it needs to, and he answers with work, lifting the sacks down. On the third day (1247.1.3 / PD 3) he carries seed to the far field last in the file of carriers, takes the outer edge of his strip against the hedge, watches the sower Alun Tredinnick's hands (his sense finds no qi in them, and finds the same faint trace of qi as in the basket-weaver in the forester, Uthno Nancarrow, who marks a tree to come down in autumn), feels watched twice and finds nothing, and takes up the last and heaviest sack alone, looking once at the forest and following the nearest pale line up the bark as far as the green screen of the *Skyveil* lets him. Bison is on the high ground above the village. In this Chapter his turns run longer and he asks a question of his own ("Is it hot?"), on the author's ruling (Evolution Entry 3, Section 14); the Seal, what Bison is, where the two come from and the old word stay unspoken and unasked. The village greets him by his bare first name and looks to him for the heavy end, and nothing in the Chapter shows anyone knowing more.

**Narrative summary (as of Chapter 006, Ref: 1.1.1.006).** At dusk on the fifteenth day of Melorany (1247.2.15 / PD 43) Hercules is at the head of the log slide with the last log of the day on his shoulder, the thick end, and Selyf Chenoweth's crew beside him; Bison is on the high ground with his face to the wind. The forest goes quiet: no bird roosts in the first trees, Yestin Hocking comes out of them saying the edge has no game, and Tegen Angwin's pigs break and run. For the first time in a year Hercules sends his sense into the forest and reads a host of twenty-eight Warrior beasts coming a long way along the edge, running together; it is across the strips and among the nearest houses before he understands what he is reading. He runs as a heavy man runs, letting nothing out, to the crossing, where the Chief is already at the hall's bell, and in the lanes he gives a strong man's share and no more: he takes a wolf off Tomas Truscott's knee and lifts the forge door's beam, turns a falling stable door over a wolf for Jenifer Pender, sets a shutter-bar against a Hart Bear whose pressure has stopped Jory Michell's nine men, and peels a wolf off Gwenna Tangye while Pasco Cobb carries her daughter to the hall; Yestin Hocking is bitten down holding a wolf pair for one long breath. In the bakery yard a Hook Wing drops on Lowen Wray and he gives up the cover in one stride: his bare back takes the hooks (***Hide Brace***), a wolf that has bitten Lowen's arm goes through the yard fence (***Heavy Palm***), Mawgan Rosewarne drags Lowen in, and Bison joins from the oven shed's roof. With the village watching from its doors and shutters the two fight with the moves the moment calls for, talk in full sentences and keep a count across the yard, and Hercules laughs for the first time in a year when Bison jokes about the ruined coat. In the mill square they finish the host (***Heavy Shove***, ***Immovable Stand***, ***Torso Guard***, ***Staggering Blow***), and Hercules's sense, reaching to count who is whole, finds a gap the size of a man at the forest's edge where a man stands in the pale lines of the trunks with his hands in his sleeves, who turns and goes into the trees; the laugh stops. In the hall he turns the smith's thanks to the craft ("The beam held because the joint was good") and with Bison asks that the work stay as it was; the Chief rules that what was seen stays in the village. On Day 17 (1247.2.17 / PD 45) he draws water at the Hedge Well, carries the village's repairs, sees it plan a watch, and holds the silhouette and the host's conduct as a fact he can name and cannot lift, and tells no one. The old weight lies on him again and the reserve is back; Bison stands beside him in the wind a long stride off.

**Narrative summary (as of Chapter 008, Ref: 1.1.1.008).** At last light on the twenty-first day of Melorany (1247.2.21 / PD 49), six days after the night of the host, Hercules hangs the yoke on its nail by the well and climbs the timber track beside the log slide with Bison to the shelf of high ground at the head of the slide, where the village has hung a field bell on a post of new ash and where Pasco Cobb's watch slate has put the two of them as a pair, so that nobody else climbs there. He turns his sense outward as the guard of the hour at the shown rank (***Champion Level 8 High***), finds no one within sight or hearing and sets the day down: the shoulders he kept narrower come down, the breath takes what it wants, the heat lies banked under the weight as it has for a year, and he sits on the flat stone beside the post with his hands open on his knees. This is the unwatched hour on a page for the first time: his voice comes out at its full length, he talks at length and asks five questions of his own, and he puts two dry lines to Bison about a pie and the gravy on a glove. He tells Bison the village six days on (the burn pit gone cold at noon; a drover who asked what had burned and was told last winter's thatch, with no second look at either of them), asks the question he carried for six days, where Bison went that night, and hears the far ford (about two dozen men in plain coats crossing by shaded lanterns with sacking-wrapped poles on their shoulders) and the dead of the bakery yard and the mill square lying with their heads to the roofs, nothing fed on. He tells his own: the gap the size of a man at the forest's edge, the man in a coat to the knee with his hands in his sleeves and no face who turned away like a man from a window, and the host's three departures, which he set beside the man as a fourth fact and told no one. Set side by side, the facts say one thing, "Someone sent them", with no name, no reason and no will in the beasts. They agree what stays (the watch as Pasco wrote it, the Chief's word, their mouths shut in the lane), and when Bison asks whether they hold to what they chose he goes still and does not answer at once. Then he answers, "We keep to it", tells what they chose on the road out (to step away from what they had been, to change who they were, to find out who they were and, in time, to find the proof that they were never guilty; no oath, and the year is how they keep it), asks "Do you want it said?", and says the fall in outline: betrayed, accused of a thing they did not do, said so and not believed, the proof made for the purpose outweighing their word, exiled. He thinks one plain line, "it was unjust", and does not follow it. He says he suspects his uncle had a hand in it; he names the Seal aloud for the first time ("My father put the Seal on me") and says he suspects his father had a use for it; the two read it together as a suspicion with no proof, that the uncle made the false proof and the fathers used it or let it stand because it served what they wanted. He says the Seal will take a few years to break and builds character, that the quiet costs a few years and more each month the village grows on them, and that the proof is a load to be set down in front of someone who does not already believe them; he asks why a rope end goes back under itself and is told. In his thought, once, he sets the true levels: he and Bison are ***Demigod Level 1 Mid*** under the shown ***Champion Level 8 High***. As the light goes he reaches for no task and lets a late feeling stand with nothing to carry it, and then he speaks the word "Olympus" once, in the voice in which he asks for water; Bison's reaction marks it, and he neither goes silent and watchful nor makes ready to leave. Nothing comes with the word. The crime, any charge, any victim, the proceeding, home, every name, title, place and polity of the fathers and the uncle, every old-tongue word and what Bison is stay unspoken.

*[L — Updated at every arc phase transition, crucible event, or status change; effective cap status updated as Olympus Suppression Seal conditions change]*

***Champion Level 8 High***; beneath it an unnamed push held down by a weight), reads Bison at the same shown rank with an unread push beneath it and Lowen Wray as Unawakened, and turns up his collar a time after Lowen Wray turns up hers; ## Section 11 — Behavioral Signature (B/L)

*[Governed by Part 39 (Character Behavioral Synthesis Law) Area 41.1. Schema: Part 37 Area 2.3. Evidence keys: the E-numbers cited in this ledger are its own numbered evidence points, the structural facts and canon citations behind its derived values (Part 39 Rule 39.45.1.2); each number belongs to this file only, so the same number in another ledger is a different point, and E1 to E7 here are not Part 5 escalation tiers.]*

**Part 39 Registration Status:** Complete — 36/36 Layers addressed, 291/291 dimensions registered, 13 Signatures
**Registration Date:** 2026-08-23
**Tier Guide (Part 39 Area 4.1):** Hero — 36 Layers, 12 Signatures, 2 contradiction pairs, 1 costly behavior (counts are guides)
**Signature Spread (Rule 39.3.3.5):** 13 Signatures across **10 distinct Layers** — L02, L03, L04, L06, L08×2, L13, L23×2, L24×2, L25, L29. **Compliant:** ≥6 distinct Layers (10), ≤3 per Layer (max 2).
**Immutable core (Rule 39.35.1.1):** **exactly 2** of the 13, author-locked — `L08-REFLEX-99` (THE PLANTING) and `L06-INFO-99` (THE WATCHING). His two defining verbs — protect it, study it. Nothing else is immutable, by explicit author decision: everything emotional (the hour, the wall, the hunger) is free to move.
**Cross-character comparison (author ruling):** none registered. Each character is registered separately; overlaps with other characters' registered values are permitted and carry no clearing, axis, or swap-test; any distinction the story needs is the author's to decide, case by case, via AskUserQuestion. No collision-axis material is recorded in this file or in the index.
**Canon item B — CLOSED, author ruling (BOTH, LAYERED):** the Firmament Lock and the Olympus Suppression Seal are both real and operate as layers — the Lock is the system-wide outer ceiling (Core Assimilation L3 Mid, over everyone in the Sol Star System), the Seal is the personal inner binding laid on him at the betrayal of Mount Olympus (Champion L8 High), and **the tighter binding governs**. `sol-star-system.md` was naming the outer ceiling correctly; it simply was not the binding constraint on him. Neither source was wrong. The authoritative model — including what neither layer touches, and that **Phase 1 breaks the Seal, not the Lock** — is owned by `ledger-worlds-location/sol-star-system.md` §2, **Layered Suppression Model**, and is not restated in this ledger. This register's Seal vocabulary is therefore the canonically correct vocabulary throughout: every conduct line below stands as registered.
**Narration rail (P30-L8, Locked):** the Empire of Gods is a FORBIDDEN ENTITY in Phase 1 prose — never named, mentioned, depicted, referenced, or alluded to, in any form, dialogue and interior monologue included. Every L34 origin cell naming Olympus below is ledger-internal and legal (Part 39 provenance lives in Section 11, never in prose — demonstrated, not named). The concealment law (§2 Permitted External Realm References, Locked) governs every vocabulary choice on the page. This rail concerns the Empire of Gods and the ledger-internal origin cells; the spoken word "Olympus" between the two exiles is the exception recorded in the block at 11.4a, and it is not the forbidden entity.

> **REGISTERED CONCEPT (author-locked) — THE MAN WHO CARRIES + THE APPRENTICE OF EVERYTHING, combined.** Sovereignty—Strength read as BEARING, not striking; institutions failed him, persons did not. And simultaneously: a god learning the ordinary world from the floor with a cultivator's total discipline, because learning is the one power no seal can cap. On Olympus he was the son and the heir; here he is nobody — and nobody is allowed to begin.

> **CANON DECISIONS (author-locked), binding on this register:**
> 1. **THE KILL AND THE CHARGES.** He has killed — multiple times, pre-story, in Olympus's service. The LATEST death is what the false charges were about — and the charges were FALSE: he did not do it, and he was shocked. He and Bison were BOTH accused. The guilt they carry is the FAILURE TO PROTECT that person, not the killing, which they did not do. The first-kill drift slot is retired as already-past (11.6 row 1); the false charge and the failure-to-protect are the L34 origin of the planting reflex and of the hearing-demand; and this is the specific content of the walled subject with Bison. P30-L8 rail: none of this may ever surface on the page — ledger-internal only. *(Author ruling, 2026-10-21, read with the second exception block at 11.4a: in the Chapter 8 hour only, an outline of this may be spoken between the two men: a betrayal, a false charge for a crime they did not commit, that they spoke against the charge and were not believed (the false evidence outweighed them), and the exile. The crime, the charge, the person who died and every detail of the proceeding stay ledger-internal and off the page.)*
> 2. **THE PERMANENTS.** Exactly two immutables: THE PLANTING and THE WATCHING — protect it, study it. Everything emotional (the hour, the wall, the hunger) is free to move.
> 3. **THE BISON SYSTEM.** THE UNWATCHED HOUR + THE UNBEARABLE SUBJECT, registered as one system (full block at 11.4a). Every day has a stretch where they are alone and he stops performing; the fall itself is walled by an agreement neither remembers making. He may say what he IS; he may not say what was DONE. Everything else between them is easy. (Read with the exception block at 11.4a: the one word "Olympus" may be spoken between the two exiles, once in Novel-Part 1, Chapter 8 and afterwards only in their private hour; what was DONE stays unsaid.) *(Author ruling, 2026-10-21: and with the second exception block at 11.4a, which opens an outline of the fall in the Chapter 8 hour only; the crime, the charge, the victim and the detail of the proceeding stay unsaid.)*
> 4. **THE PRESSURE SYSTEM.** HEAT AND STILLNESS + HUNGER FOR THE CEILING, registered as one system (block in 11.1 at L04). Constant physical pressure managed with breath, stillness and work; and the pressure expresses as appetite for the heaviest possible work, subtly addictive.
> 5. **THE FIGHT SYSTEM.** All four grammars — POSITIONAL; ONE BLOW, ENDED; CONTROLS WITHOUT HARMING; ESCALATING IN AUDITED STEPS — chosen as the situation demands; the mode-choice is itself the conduct (block in 11.1 at L25).
> 6. **THE SPLIT READ.** Ordinary people read him as KIND AND UNREMARKABLE — the exact disguise he needs, and it costs him daily, because being small is the price of being safe. Cultivators and power-sensitive people read A MAN RUNNING FROM SOMETHING — they correctly sense a past and guess wrong at its contents. Paired with the flicker watch: their reaction is exactly what he is scanning for (11.7).
> 7. **ZEUS AS STANDARD.** The father never appears as thought or word — he appears as a STANDARD. Hercules watches every powerful person against a measure he has never named, and almost nobody passes. The one man who might have deserved his loyalty is the one who let the charges stand. *(Author ruling, 2026-10-21, read with the second exception block at 11.4a: in the Chapter 8 hour only, Hercules says "my father" (no name, no "Son of", no title, no place, no polity), says that his father placed the Seal on him, and suspects, with no proof, a hidden agenda behind it. The father still never appears as a figure, a voice or a quoted word, and the unnamed standard stands.)*
> 8. **THE CAUSE (P30-L3).** THE CHILDREN + THE NAME, CHANGED. Purpose found in the young protagonists — protector by instinct, not appointment: they are the people most likely to be failed by the powerful, and he has met the powerful. And the mission to clear the charges, transformed: no longer restoration but the truth existing at all — for the person who died, for Bison, for the record. He would settle for nobody ever knowing, so long as it is true.
> 9. **THE ARC LOCKS.** First significant defeat = HE PUSHES AT THE CAP (permanent; it is what begins the fracture). First PIS-5 = HE TRIES TO TEAR THE SEAL OPEN (the worst hour: a man discovering that the one thing he cannot carry, out-work or out-learn is what was done to him). The Locked S6/S7 cap-lift = IT SHARPENS THE QUESTION (exile turns from a condition into a PROBLEM; the apprentice attacks it). Loss of Bison = THE CARRYING TURNS COMPULSIVE (permanent). All mapped at 11.6.

### 11.1 — Layer Register

- `B39-L25-OPEN-99` — **THE FOUR GRAMMARS (system anchor): he opens by choosing the grammar the situation demands — positional, the single blow, the harmless control, or the audited climb; the mode-choice is itself the conduct** `[ASKED]` *(author-locked, FIGHT; the full system block follows)*

> **The Fight System (L25, author-locked — all four grammars registered; the mode-choice is itself the conduct):**
> - **POSITIONAL** — takes a doorway, a bridge, the space in front of people, and makes going through him impossible; occupies rather than duels (carriers: `L25-OPEN-99`, `L25-FOOT-99`, `L25-PROTECT-99`).
> - **ONE BLOW, ENDED** — a single placed strike, because a second blow means the first was misjudged (carrier: `L25-TEMPO-99`).
> - **CONTROLS WITHOUT HARMING** — grapples, immobilizes, holds people still; harm avoided from CAUTION, because he does not know what he would do if he stopped being careful (carriers: `L25-TARGET-99`, `L25-MERCY-99`).
> - **ESCALATING IN AUDITED STEPS** — opens far below the ceiling, adds exactly what is proven necessary, one increment at a time; the terrifying implication to a close observer is that the steps have never yet run out (carrier: `L25-ESCAL-99`, Signature).
> *Part 5 combat law, Part 3 realm physics, and the enforced Champion L8 High ceiling (E4) constrain absolutely throughout; expression may never exceed the cap, save the one bounded Section 8 exception of Part 30 Law P30-L1 (a single Catastrophic-type move through his true Demigod cultivation, which the Seal punishes).*

- `B39-L25-ESCAL-99` — **THE AUDITED STEPS: opens far below the ceiling, adds exactly what is proven necessary, one increment at a time — and the steps have never yet run out** `[ASKED]` `[SIGNATURE]` *(author-locked, FIGHT grammar 4; the staged-revelation arc S2→S4 (E19, E21) is this conduct on the page; the external cap (E4) is the one escalation limit that is not his choice)*

### 11.3 — Contradiction and Cost (B)

**Registered theme:** ***the man who carries and the apprentice of everything — strength as bearing, learning as the one uncappable power; institutions failed him, persons did not.***

**Contradiction Pair CP-1 — STEPPING FORWARD vs STAYING HIDDEN** `[ASKED]`
`B39-L08-REFLEX-99` (the planting — **immutable**; the body goes between) versus `B39-L02-THREAT-99` with the concealment law (recognition is the threat; E11/E12).
**Collides:** every interposition makes him memorable, and a man concealing what he is cannot afford to be seen. It fires every time he protects someone — the reflex spends exactly the currency the cover hoards.

**Contradiction Pair CP-2 — LEARNING vs BELONGING** `[ASKED]`
`B39-L06-INFO-99` (the watching — **immutable**; the apprentice's whole method) versus `B39-L13-MASK-99` / `L21-KNOWN-99` (the constructed ordinary man the village is fond of).
**Collides:** studying a place is not being from it; the better he passes, the more precisely he knows he is performing. Competence and loneliness grow at the same rate.

**Contradiction Pair CP-3 — THE LOAD vs THE CEILING** `[ASKED]`
`B39-L24-PLATEAU-99` (the hunger — the heaviest work as the only relief) versus `B39-L25-ESCAL-99` / `L11-GAME-99` (the margin that must never show).
**Collides:** the heaviest work is both who he is and his only relief, and doing it at full permitted strength performs something no ordinary villager could. Relief and exposure are the same act.

**Costly Behavior — `B39-L36-COST-99`, THE LOADS THAT ARE NOT HIS.** `[ASKED]`
He shoulders burdens nobody asked him to — others' work, blame, danger — and pays in time, suspicion, exhaustion and attention a hidden man cannot afford. And he cannot stop learning: the appetite for how things work pulls him where a man in hiding should not linger, and he gets remembered. **Costs him:** the anonymity that keeps him and Bison alive, spent daily in small denominations; and the hours — the strongest back in the valley is also its most over-lent. **Paid on the page at:** 1.1.1.002, the first payment, at survivable scale: in the lane (Area block 04) the lane's eyes land on him and stay a beat too long after he stops the loaded cart and puts the one question, and he answers the nearness with work. **Paid again at 1.1.1.006 (Gate 2 of 2026-10-20):** in Area block 02 (the beam, the stable door, the shutter-bar and the wolf thrown off a smith's knee, each a load that was not his, with the smith's eyes staying on his two hands "longer than a man should" after the lift) and in Area block 06 in the hours of the repairs (the door frame, a roof beam, forty planks and the first of the posts, each "a strong man's load and no more").

**Sub-Optimal Tendency — `B39-L36-SUBOPT-99`:** the interposition error — he intervenes physically when a word would have served, because standing in front of a problem is easier than talking about it; and the understanding error — he prioritizes understanding a situation over acting on it, and has watched a moment pass while working out how it functioned. `[ASKED]`
**Off-Register Allowance — `B39-L36-OFFREG-99`:** occurs only inside the unwatched hour, before Bison; marked by Bison's reaction, never his own. Used this Section: **yes** (set at the Gate 2 of 2026-10-22: the word was written on the page at 1.1.1.008, spoken once by Hercules as the last beat of the unwatched hour and marked by Bison's reaction; the single Section 1 act is his and is spent, and Bison's allowance is not spent by it). The one spoken word "Olympus" in Novel-Part 1, Chapter 8 spends the speaker's single Section 1 off-register act; "Used this Section" stayed "no" until that word was written on the page and is now set to yes. See the block at 11.4a. *(Author ruling, 2026-10-21: Hercules speaks the word, so the act is his; see the second exception block at 11.4a.)*

### 11.3a — Owed Beats (L)

*[One line per beat a rule owes this character, in the form rule · code · shown in Area NN or OPEN. Opened with the registration and written at Gate 2 by the Post-Write Ledger Update (Part 39 Rule 39.41.5.2).]*

| Rule | Code | Shown in Area |
|---|---|---|
| Rule 39.12.1.2, unobserved conduct: a character above Tier C is shown behaving when they believe themselves unobserved | `B39-L08-UNSEEN-99` | Shown in 1.1.1.008 |
| Rule 39.17.1.1, the mask shown both sides | `B39-L13-MASK-99` | Shown in 1.1.1.006 |
| Rule 39.22.1.1, the oath rendered at its cost | `B39-L18-WEIGHT-99` | Shown in 1.1.1.008 |

*Opened at the first Post-Write Ledger Update to read this file (Part 39 Rule 39.41.5.2), which was the Gate 2 of the first draft of Chapter 002; the notes that update and the next one wrote about that draft's two Areas were rolled back 2026-10-07 with the archived Areas. The four lines above are owed by the registered codes; at that update none of them was entered as shown. The two other beat rules owe him nothing: his humour line is `B39-L11-MECH-99`, not the `B39-L11-MECH-10` of Rule 39.15.1.1, and `B39-L19-SEAM-01` is not applicable while no code binds him (Section 11.5). Each line stays OPEN until an Area shows it. The Section-close report lists any still OPEN as information only; no beat is added to a scene to clear it. At the Gate 2 of the whole build of Chapter 002 (2026-10-08) the costly-behavior line was entered as shown in 1.1.1.002: the Chapter Spec planned the first payment for the lane (Area block 04), where the attention of the lane lands on him and stays after he stops the loaded cart and puts the one question, and he answers it with work. The other three lines stay OPEN, as the Chapter plans none of them: the well scene at first light holds only the two men for a while, with no old word and no word of where they come from (the unwatched hour waits for Chapter 008), so the mask is not shown lifted, no unobserved conduct is shown, and no oath is rendered at its cost.* *At the Gate 2 of the whole build of Chapter 006 (2026-10-20) the mask line was entered as shown in 1.1.1.006, because the Chapter Spec planned it across Area blocks 03 and 06 and the written text shows both faces: the cover lifted before the village (the year "went out of him with that stride as a latch drops", Area block 03; the laughter and the banter, Area blocks 03 and 04) and the cover back on Day 17 (the old weight on him again and the quiet and the stillness, Area block 06). The costly-behavior line stays as first shown in 1.1.1.002 and is paid again in 1.1.1.006 (the saves of Area block 02 and the hours of the repairs in Area block 06). The unobserved-conduct line stays OPEN: his one hour alone at the hedge on Day 17 has a villager at the well within sight, so he is not shown behaving as one who believes himself unobserved (Area block 06). The oath line stays OPEN. No beat is added to a scene to clear a line.* *At the Gate 2 of the whole build of Chapter 008 (2026-10-22) the unobserved-conduct line was entered as shown in 1.1.1.008: the Chapter Spec planned it, and the page shows him setting the day down only after his sense has found no one ("No one was within sight or hearing, and no one was coming"), with Bison, the one person the concealment law exempts, beside him in the unwatched hour. The oath line `B39-L18-WEIGHT-99` stays OPEN: the page renders a promise kept at a named cost ("It costs a few years of the quiet, with our hands kept small, and the waiting, and it costs more each month that the village grows on us"), but it states that "No oath was said on that road, and none has been said since", and Rule 39.22.1.1 owes the oath rendered at its cost. The Chapter Spec's plan counted the promise; the author may accept it for that rule, and nothing was entered on the plan alone. **Ruled by the author (2026-10-22, after the Gate 2): the promise is accepted for Rule 39.22.1.1, and the oath line is entered as shown in 1.1.1.008, to match Bison's `B39-L18-WEIGHT-05`; the "stays OPEN" reading in this paragraph is superseded by that ruling.** The mask line stays as first shown in 1.1.1.006, and the costly-behavior line as first shown in 1.1.1.002 and paid again in 1.1.1.006 (the Chapter does not engage `B39-L36-COST-99`). No beat is added to a scene to clear a line.*
