### 11.4a — Relational Behavior Matrix (Layer L14)

**Hercules → Bison (best friend; the register's deepest bond — the last witness).** `L14-DELTA-99` THE UNWATCHED HOUR: with Bison alone the concealment stands down — vocabulary, memory, identity all legal (E11); he is audibly and behaviorally *from somewhere* only in that company, and the performing stops. `L14-TRIGGER-99` shared origin and shared fall: the only witness to his whole self (E3, E14) — and the co-accused (canon decision 1). `L14-PHYSICAL` he checks Bison's position in any room before anything else, and has never once announced it. `L14-FORBIDDEN-99` **THE UNBEARABLE SUBJECT: the fall itself — "Olympus" stays on this list, with ONE exception: the word is spoken once between the two in Novel-Part 1, Chapter 8, as the one wall-crossing word, and afterwards ONLY in their private hour (the unwatched hour with no witness), never in company, never in front of anyone; see the exception block after the Bison System below;** **the charges, the death, home — walled by an agreement neither remembers making. He may say what he IS; he may not say what was DONE. Everything else between them is easy.** *(Author ruling, 2026-10-21: and with the second exception block after the Bison System below, which opens an outline of the fall in the Chapter 8 hour only.)* `L14-STABILITY-99` under canonical pressure — Part 30 pulls the two arcs deliberately apart (Bison must individuate); the bond's delta belongs to the drift map, and its loss is drift row 6. `L14-WITNESS-99` in any in-system company the concealment resumes for both (E11): the friendship visibly reduces to its cover version. *(See Evolution Entry 2, Section 14: one short, flat, dry exchange of the two men is added to the cover version in company, ruled 2026-10-07.)*

> **The Bison System (L14/L23/L09, author-locked — both halves registered as one system):**
> - **THE UNWATCHED HOUR** — every day has a stretch where they are alone and he stops performing: speaks the old vocabulary, names what he is, says what the concealment law forbids everywhere else; the only hour he is not lying by omission; guarded by both without ever being agreed (carriers: `L23-ROUTINE-99` anchor and Signature, `L14-DELTA-99`, `L09-MECH-99`, `L21-AFTER-99`, `L19-PLACE-99`).
> - **THE UNBEARABLE SUBJECT** — the fall itself — "Olympus" stays on this list, with ONE exception: the word is spoken once between the two in Novel-Part 1, Chapter 8, as the one wall-crossing word, and afterwards ONLY in their private hour (the unwatched hour with no witness), never in company, never in front of anyone; see the exception block below; the charges, the death, home — walled by an agreement neither remembers making; he may say what he IS, not what was DONE; everything else between them is easy (carriers: `L14-FORBIDDEN-99`, `L07-FORBID-99`, `L09-CONCEAL-99`; content: canon decision 1). *(Author ruling, 2026-10-21: and see the second exception block below.)*
> *Narration rails: realm vocabulary is admissible inside the hour (E11); the Empire of Gods is not, ever (P30-L8). The matching half of this system is registered at Bison's own Part 39 interview — nothing here pre-decides his side.*

> ### ONE WORD CROSSES THE WALL: SPOKEN ONCE IN CHAPTER 8, AFTERWARDS ONLY IN THEIR PRIVATE HOUR, NEVER IN COMPANY; EVERY OTHER WALL STANDS. `[ASKED]`
>
> **Settled.** The word "Olympus" is spoken once between Hercules and Bison, in the unwatched hour of Novel-Part 1, Chapter 8 ("The Hour Nobody Watches", the hour with no witness), as the one wall-crossing word in that hour, and afterwards may be spoken between them only in their private hour. The word is otherwise inside the walled subject, so it is a narrow exception to `B39-L14-FORBIDDEN-99`, to bullet 2 of the Bison System block and to the mirrored sentence in canon decision 3 at the head of Section 11; every other wall is kept. The same exception is recorded in `bison.md`.
>
> **What it allows.** The word "Olympus" is spoken once between the two in Part 1's hour (Novel-Part 1, Chapter 8, the hour with no witness), as the one wall-crossing word, and afterwards may be spoken between the two ONLY in their private hour (the unwatched hour, alone together, no witness), never in company and never in front of anyone. At every instance the rule that follows holds. The other man's reaction marks it, never the speaker's own marking. The ruling does not fix which of the two speaks it. It is the word only: no place, people, title, event or reason comes with it.
>
> **What stays walled.** Every other wall. What was DONE stays unsaid: no charge, proceeding, trial, verdict, banishment, betrayal or death is named, and no reason for the banishment is given; they may say what they ARE. No father's name ("Zeus", "Nandi", "Son of ..."). No old word is spelled, coined or spoken without an author ruling: the closed permit list and the exhaustive never-list in §1 element 3's rail guard stand as written, and the author ruled none in Part 1. "Olympus" is not a Lexicon B word and does not pass through that permit; it is the one word the P30-L8 exception lets be spoken. The Empire of Gods and every other name for the origin stay barred. Realm vocabulary stays under §2. The exception is for the hour with no witness only: in any in-system company the concealment resumes for both (`B39-L14-WITNESS-99`). After Chapter 8 the word is licensed in the private hour only, the unwatched hour with no witness, never in company and never in front of anyone: when anyone is present the concealment resumes for both (`B39-L14-WITNESS-99`) and the word is not spoken. The limit is the place, the private hour; no count and no end point is set. The word alone is carried, and every wall named above stands for every instance. It does not make the subject speakable. *(Author ruling, 2026-10-21: superseded in part, for the Chapter 8 hour only, by the second exception block below, which opens an outline of the fall; the walls it does not name stand as written here.)*
>
> **Settled and open.** (1) **Off-register act: SETTLED for the first instance.** The one spoken word "Olympus" in Novel-Part 1, Chapter 8 spends the speaker's single Section 1 off-register act (`B39-L36-OFFREG-99`, Rule 39.40.2.4: one per Section). The ruling does not fix which of the two speaks the word; the act spent is the speaker's own register's, so if Hercules speaks it, it is this one. "Used this Section" stays "no" in 11.3 until the word is written on the page; a note beside the field and beside `L36-OFFREG-99` records that the act is committed to that word, and the field is then set to yes. Later private-hour instances, and whether they count against the single act or sit outside it, are not decided. (2) **Reversion trigger: OPEN, deferred to Chapter Spec (Novel-Part 1, Chapter 8).** `B39-L31-REVERT-99` (anything touching the charges or Olympus returns him to the arrival self) is unchanged in text and is marked `[OPEN: deferred to Chapter Spec (Novel-Part 1, Chapter 8)]`. Until the Chapter Spec rules, this file does not say whether the spoken word fires it, and the same holds for later private-hour instances. *(Author ruling, 2026-10-21: item (1) is fixed, since Hercules speaks the word, and item (2) is answered for the one spoken word, which does not fire the trigger; see the second exception block below. The rest of item (2) stays open.)*
>
> **What is not touched.** Every other statement of the wall in this file stands unchanged and is read with this one exception: canon decisions 1 and 3; `B39-L07-FORBID-99`; `B39-L09-CONCEAL-99`; `B39-L09-HOLD-99`; the 11.4 row for the unwatched hour; §1 element 3's rail guard (the closed permit list, the never-list and the OPEN CANON note); the Section 11 header prose rail and every L34 origin cell, which concern the Empire of Gods and the provenance of the fall and not this spoken word. `B39-L23-ROUTINE-99` and its text are unchanged.
>
> *First used on a page at 1.1.1.008 (2026-10-22): Hercules speaks the word "Olympus" once, as the last act of the unwatched hour, in the voice in which he asks for water, with nothing coming with it ("Nothing came with it. It was a word, and the lamps below were lamps."). Bison's reaction marks it, and Hercules neither goes silent and watchful nor makes ready to leave. No later instance is written.*

> ### THE WALL OF THE HOUR, OPENED A LITTLE IN CHAPTER 8: AN OUTLINE OF THE FALL, IN THAT HOUR ONLY, BETWEEN THE TWO MEN ONLY; EVERY OTHER WALL STANDS. `[ASKED]`
>
> **Settled.** *Author ruling, 2026-10-21 (Chapter Spec 1.1.1.008, "The Hour Nobody Watches", its Settled notes, as relayed by the orchestrating command; written in the pre-draft ledger step of 2026-10-22, before the Chapter is drafted; nothing below is on a page yet).* In the unwatched hour of Novel-Part 1, Chapter 8 (Ref: 1.1.1.008), and in that hour only, Hercules and Bison may speak to each other, in outline, of the fall. This is a second narrow exception, beside the exception for the one word "Olympus" above, which stands as written. It is an exception to `B39-L14-FORBIDDEN-99`, to bullet 2 of the Bison System block, to canon decisions 1, 3 and 7 at the head of Section 11, and to the lines of this file that say the father never appears as thought or word or that the imposer of the Seal stays unnamed. Both exceptions are read inside the exception sentence of Law P30-L8 as it stands (the word Olympus, the Seal, the reasons for the banishment and the promise to clear the name). The author ruled that no Canon Amendment is made for the words "my father" and "my uncle"; the reading of that sentence is left to the checker's audit and to Gate 1 Check #11 element 11-3 (Chapter Spec 1.1.1.008, Open). The same exception is recorded in `bison.md`.
>
> **What it allows.** Only in the hour: two men alone, no third person within sight or hearing, and the concealment resumes the moment anyone is present (`B39-L14-WITNESS-99`).
> 1. **The word "Olympus", spoken once, by Hercules.** Who speaks it, left open above, is now fixed. The other man's reaction marks it, never the speaker's own marking.
> 2. **The Seal, named.** It is a named item (set in italic in prose; one row in `ledger-named-items`) and may be spoken or thought in the hour.
> 3. **The fall, in outline:** a brief word of their time there; a betrayal; being framed for a crime they did not commit; that they spoke against the charge and were not believed (the fabricated evidence outweighed them); and the exile.
> 4. **What they decided:** to step away, to change who they are, to find themselves and, in time, to find proof of their innocence. The promise is held by conduct and no oath is spoken as an oath.
> 5. **The uncle, Hercules only:** he says he suspects "my uncle" (his own uncle, unnamed, with no title and no place) arranged the false evidence. See the uncle entry in Section 7.
> 6. **The fathers, both men:** each says "my father" (no name, no "Son of", no title, no place, no polity and no name for the origin) and that his own father placed the Seal on him, and the two share a suspicion that the fathers had a hidden agenda and used the exile. The reading the talk reaches: the uncle arranged the false evidence; the fathers used it, or let it stand, because it served their plan; the exile was unjust as to the charge, and necessary to the fathers' plan. There is no proof either way, and it stays a suspicion.
> 7. **The Seal as they take it:** a binding that takes "a few years" to break (no number, no mechanism and no condition of breaking) and that is grounding and builds character. This is the exiles' belief. It sets no ledger fact and moves no Phase 1 end-state, no band and no Drift Map row; the record of Section 2 and the model in `sol-star-system.md` stand.
> 8. **The true level, once:** his true level (Demigod Level 1 Mid, pressed under the shown Champion Level 8 High) and Bison's true level (Demigod Level 1 Mid, as `bison.md` Section 2 holds it) are stated once, as designations, in Hercules's interior thought, under the self-identity permission of Section 2 (the Chapter Spec's Settled note allows, instead, Bison saying his own once in speech). The word "Demigod" is lawful in the hour only.
> 9. **The bare word "unjust",** carried once, as one plain line or thought: a narrow carve-out in the manner of the word "Olympus". It carries no event, charge, reason, name or old word with it.
> 10. **Address:** Bison calls Hercules by the bare name, "Hercules" (his side is registered in `bison.md`); Hercules uses Bison's bare name as registered.
>
> **What stays walled.** Every other wall. The crime itself; any charge; any victim or death; any detail of the proceeding (who judged it, who was present, where it was held, what was said in it); home (the word is not said); every name, title, place and polity of either father and of the uncle (no "Zeus", no "Nandi", no "Son of ...", no title, no place, no Empire of Gods and no other name for the origin); every old word (the closed permit list and the never-list of §1 element 3's rail guard stand as written and govern old words only; "my father", "my uncle" and the outline are plain words, and the author ruled no old word for Part 1); Bison's form and species (no horn, tail, bull head, species word or startled oath); the mechanism of the Seal's making or breaking and any number beyond "a few years"; and any second use of the word "Olympus" in Chapter 8 itself (the earlier exception, for later private hours, stands as written above). The father still never appears as a figure, a voice or a quoted word, and the unnamed standard of canon decision 7 stands, inward and unnamed. The forbidden thought of `B39-L07-FORBID-99` (the replay of the proceeding to its end) stays forbidden: the outline is not the replay. The exception is for the hour with no witness only; in any company the concealment resumes for both.
>
> **Settled and open.** (1) **Off-register act: Hercules speaks the word, so the act is his** (`B39-L36-OFFREG-99`, Rule 39.40.2.4: one per Section); Bison's allowance is not spent by it. "Used this Section" stays "no" in 11.3 until the word is written on the page and is then set to yes, at that Chapter's Gate 2. Later private-hour instances are not decided. (2) **Reversion trigger `B39-L31-REVERT-99`: ANSWERED for the one spoken word, which does NOT fire it.** In the hour Hercules neither goes silent and watchful nor makes ready to leave at the word, and the stillness after it is the hour's. STILL OPEN, not ruled: whether talk of the charges beyond the word, or a later private-hour instance of the word, fires it. The talk of the charges falls to the Chapter's own Check #21 at its Gate 1, where the author rules with the Area text printed. (3) **Check #21 (Part 39 Area 44; Part 43).** The opening of the wall is a change beyond the registered values for both men. Any `[EVOLVE]` or other ruling is owed to the Gate 1 of Chapter 8. Nothing is written to Section 12 or Section 14 here, no Drift Count moves and no Layer line is edited.
>
> **What this ruling amends, each spot marked "Author ruling, 2026-10-21".** (a) The origin cell of Signature 6 (`B39-L08-POWERLESS-99`) at 11.2: the earlier reading, that no word of theirs was heard, is replaced by the ruling that he and Bison spoke against the false charge and were not believed. "The hearing he never got" (Section 1 element 1(b), the Layer line and the Signature row) and "the proceeding he never got" (`B39-L13-BETRAY-99`) stay as registered conduct and are read as a hearing or proceeding that would have weighed his answer and checked the evidence; no Signature text is rewritten. (b) Dated pointers sit at: Section 1 element 1(b) and the old-word never-list of element 3; the Antagonists line, the Zeus line and the address rows for his father in Section 7; canon decisions 1, 3 and 7; the Layer lines `B39-L07-FORBID-99`, `B39-L08-POWERLESS-99`, `B39-L13-UP-99`, `B39-L13-BETRAY-99`, `B39-L26-LOSS-99`, `B39-L31-REVERT-99` (answered in part) and `B39-L34-END`; the origin cells of Signatures 4, 6 (amended in place) and 11; the 11.4 row for the unwatched hour; the `B39-L36-OFFREG-99` Layer line and the 11.3 field; and, at 11.4a, the paragraph for Bison, the Bison System block, the first exception block and the Hercules to Zeus line. (c) Section 7 gains an entry for the uncle, under Notable NPCs.
>
> **What is not touched.** Every other statement of the wall in this file stands as written and is read with these two exceptions: `B39-L09-CONCEAL-99`; `B39-L09-HOLD-99` (held from everyone in-system except Bison, and the concealment resumes in company); the 11.4 rows other than the unwatched hour; §1 element 3's rail guard (the closed permit list, the never-list and the OPEN CANON note); the Section 11 header prose rail and every L34 origin cell, which concern the Empire of Gods and the provenance of the fall and not this speech; the Phase 1 end-state, the bands and the Drift Map, which the exiles' belief about the Seal does not move; and Law P30-L8 itself, which is not amended. `B39-L23-ROUTINE-99` and its text are unchanged.
>
> *First used on a page at 1.1.1.008 (2026-10-22): the opening of this block is written in the unwatched hour of the Chapter and in that hour only, with no third person within sight or hearing (his sense had found none). On the page: the outline of the fall; the bare word "unjust" (once, as one plain thought); "my uncle" (a suspicion with no proof); "my father" and the Seal named aloud for the first time (his father placed it on him; he suspects, with no proof, that his father had a use for it, and says of both fathers that they "used it, or let it stand, because it served what they wanted"); the Seal as a binding that will take a few years to break and builds character (the two men's belief, which sets no ledger fact); and the true level set once, in his thought. The author ruled the Check #21 enquiry `[EVOLVE]` (ADD) on 2026-10-22: Evolution Entries 9 to 12, Section 14. The reversion trigger did not fire (Entry 10); talk of the charges beyond the outline, and later private-hour instances of the word, stay open. Every wall this block names as standing stands, and nothing of them is on the page.*

**Hercules → Zeus (father).** `L14-DELTA-01` not applicable in-Phase: Zeus is not present and may not surface (P30-L8); the relationship's whole behavioral residue is registered at `L13-UP-99` — the father never appears as thought or word; he appears as a STANDARD (canon decision 7). `L14-TRIGGER` / `L14-PHYSICAL` / `L14-FORBIDDEN` reserved — Phase 2+ material, not invented here. `L14-STABILITY-01` static in-Phase (broken by banishment, §7 — "deeply complicated" left undefined by canon). `L14-WITNESS-01` not applicable. *(Author ruling, 2026-10-21, read with the second exception block above: in the Chapter 8 hour only, Hercules says "my father" to Bison, says that his father placed the Seal on him and suspects, with no proof, a hidden agenda behind it. Zeus is still not present and the name may not surface (P30-L8); he never appears as a figure, a voice or a quoted word.)*

**Hercules → Nandi (Bison's father; known associate from Olympus).** `L14-DELTA` reserved — §7 records the association only; no conduct invented. All cells `[N-A]` at current establishment; rows accrue if canon activates him.

**Hercules → the young protagonists (S4+, as a class).** `L14-DELTA-99` THE CHILDREN governs (`L23-CHILD-99`): the edge-of-scene planting; attempts permitted, falling forbidden. `L14-TRIGGER-99` the locked cause: they are the people most likely to be failed by the powerful (canon decision 8; P30-L3). `L14-PHYSICAL` a place beside him at work — the child who wants to watch finds the task angled so they can see his hands. `L14-FORBIDDEN` reserved per person; individual rows accrue from S4 (E21) via Section 12. `L14-STABILITY-99` growing — this class is where the P30-L3 arc lands. `L14-WITNESS-01` unchanged by company.

**Hercules → the village (as a class).** `L14-DELTA-99` the gentle giant register: useful, quiet, restful to work near, studied by nobody and studying everyone. `L14-TRIGGER-99` the cover, grown warm: what began as disguise has begun to be affection, and he has not noticed the seam move (CP-2). `L14-PHYSICAL` the doorways he fills and the loads he takes are the village's map of him. `L14-FORBIDDEN` every personal question — deflected into work, never answered false (`L09-MECH-99`). `L14-STABILITY-99` deepening across S1–S5 (E18–E23). `L14-WITNESS-01` unchanged.

**Hercules → parents (other) / mentors / rivals:** `[N-A]` — none established (§7); entries reserved, not invented.

### 11.5 — Collective Code Binding (B/L)

**Active collective codes:** none. No faction, sect, rank, or registered village Collective Behavior Code binds him (E10); he is the border village's guest, not its member — the permanent outsider of the entry-point mandate (E16).
**Conformance — `L19-CONFORM-01`:** not applicable at current establishment; his scrupulous local-rule keeping is cover conduct (`L06-RULE-99`), not code membership.
**Deviation seam — `L19-SEAM-01`:** not applicable while no code binds.
**Personal law note:** the one code-like structure he does obey is the concealment law itself (§2, Locked) — a personal legal architecture, not a collective code; its conduct is registered across L09 and L13, not here.
**Forward note (planned, not executed):** S5 guild integration (E23 — "guilds that do not care about cultural origin, only capability") makes this Layer live mid-Phase. When it fires: register the guild code, his conformance posture, and the seam — the predicted seam is the audited-steps man inside a capability culture that rewards display. Enters via Section 12 under Check #21 or a planned drift entry at that Section.

### 11.7 — Read-By-Others Registry (Layer L33) (B/L)

**Noticed first:** the size, then the quiet of it (`L33-FIRST-99`) — an enormous man who makes no noise and watches your hands.
**Routinely misread as (`L33-MISREAD-99`, author-locked):** **ordinary people read him as KIND AND UNREMARKABLE** — a gentle giant, well-liked, trusted with children, mildly pitied. The specific wrong conclusion (Rule 39.37.1.2): that the gentleness is the whole of him. It is the exact disguise he needs, and it costs him daily — being small is the price of being safe.
**By rank (`L33-BYRANK-99`, author-locked):** *villagers and commons* read the gentle giant, truly as far as it goes — the kindness is real. ***Cultivators and power-sensitive people* read A MAN RUNNING FROM SOMETHING** — they correctly sense a past and guess wrong at its contents; their reaction is exactly what the flicker watch is scanning for (`L02-THREAT-99`). *Rulers*, from S4: potential asset, potential danger, eventually potential ally (E21). *Animals and non-baseline senses* register something a human might not notice (E22, Part 30 S4 canon) — his side of that beat is the flicker watch and the exit; this entry supplies, from his side, the matching half of the Part 30 Section 4 mandate that `aegon.md` §11.1 quotes at `B39-L02-PEOPLE-07` (recorded here as canon consistency only; no comparison material is derived).
**By intimates (`L33-INTIMATE-99`):** Bison holds the only complete reading in the system (E14): he knows what the carrying is for — that it is not generosity, it is how Hercules keeps from thinking.
**Reputation gap (`L33-REP-99`):** structural and enormous — the village's strong protector, then "big outlander" fame beyond the village from S2, then "formidable outlander" to the rulers from S4 (E21), all of it resting on a suppressed Demigod's conduct (E4). He manages the widening gap by getting smaller as the stories get bigger.
**The deep reading as a read (`L24-SENSE-99`):** nobody reads him reading them — he knows more about everyone's cultivation than anyone suspects and acts, permanently, as if he does not.
**Last revised:** 2026-08-23 (registration).

*[L — 11.1 non-signature lines, 11.4, 11.4a, 11.5 and 11.7 are Living. 11.2, 11.3 and 11.6 are Baseline.]*

## Section 13 — Current Condition (L)

*[Detailed form (Part 37 Area 2.1). Every field reads `none recorded at story start` until an Area changes it.]*

**Emotional state:** Grounded at the close of the unwatched hour of Chapter 008 (Ref: 1.1.1.008), on the evening of Day 21 (1247.2.21 / PD 49): the six days of carrying one question and one sight alone are over (he has asked, told and been told, and "the six days went out of the air between them without a mark"); Bison's question whether they hold to what they chose put the stillness on him "from the hands inward", and he has answered "We keep to it", said the fall in outline and spent the word "Olympus", with the other man beside him. At the end of the hour a late feeling, unnamed, "a tightness in the throat that was not the cold, and arms that remembered the shape of a beam and wanted the heaviest end of it", is let stand with nothing to carry it: "it did not break him, and it did not ask to be carried either" (ruled in Evolution Entry 12, Section 14). *Earlier, as of Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006):* Quiet and still at the close of Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006): at dusk on Day 17 (1247.2.17 / PD 45), alone at the hedge with a villager at the well within sight, "he found he had gone quiet and still, and let himself" (Area block 06). Joyful in the village's sight on the night of Day 15 (1247.2.15 / PD 43): the first laugh in a year in the bakery yard at the coat, and a second in the mill square, with "the laughter still in his breath" and his friend at his back (Area blocks 03 and 04), until his sense reached the forest's edge and found the gap the size of a man (Area block 04); stressed in the run and the saves of the lanes (Area blocks 01 and 02). *Earlier, as of Chapter 002 (Ref: 1.1.1.002):* settled and absorbed in the work at the close of the Chapter: with the last and heaviest sack of the strip taken up alone, his shoulders loosen under it as another man's might in a chair (Day 3, 1247.1.3 / PD 3, Ref: 1.1.1.002). Earlier: easy and slow in the cover when the village greeted him, and still and held for one breath under the weight at Kitto Hale's cask (Day 1); gone quiet when the carter blamed the lad in the lane (Day 2).
**Mental state:** At the close of Chapter 008 (Ref: 1.1.1.008) he knows what Bison alone held for six days (the dead of the bakery yard and the mill square lying with their heads to the roofs, no hen touched, no sack opened, nothing fed on; about two dozen men in plain coats with sacking-wrapped poles crossing a ford beyond the forest by shaded lanterns before the grey, whether coming or going unknown), and Bison knows what he held (the gap the size of a man, the man in a coat to the knee with his hands in his sleeves and no face, and the host's three departures set beside him as a fourth fact); each kept it from the other and neither blames. The two read the host as sent ("Someone sent them"), with no name, no reason, no kingdom and no will in the beasts, and agree what stays: the watch as Pasco wrote it, the Chief's word and their mouths shut in the lane. He holds the fall in outline as a suspicion with no proof (his uncle made the false proof, and the fathers "used it, or let it stand, because it served what they wanted"); he holds the Seal as a binding that will take a few years to break; he holds the proof as "a load" to be carried to someone "who does not already believe us" and does not know to whom, what it weighs or where it is kept. He knows now how a rope end is whipped (he asked, and was told), and he has set his own true level and Bison's in his thought. The village has seen none of it: a drover who asked what had burned was told last winter's thatch and gave no second look. *Earlier, as of Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006), before he told Bison:* At the close of Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006) he holds, and tells no one, four facts (Area block 06): wolves, bears and foxes, "which keep to their own, had run as one"; "They had not fed. They had not turned back."; and a man stood at the edge of the trees with his hands in his sleeves "and nothing in him that a sense could find". He knows that the village watched him at full measure from its doors and shutters and that the Chief ruled that what was seen stays in the village (Area blocks 03 and 05); on Day 17 he watches the village's look at his back for "the catch in it, the second glance", finds it, and finds it put away (Area block 06). His sense has read the host's six Kinds as worded ranges and the villagers by level, and found nothing in the man at the forest's edge (Area blocks 01 to 04). *Earlier, as of Chapter 002 (Ref: 1.1.1.002):* attentive to hands and work: he watches the sower Alun Tredinnick's hands and would have watched another strip's length of it, and counts Alun's swings as he pours seed into the bag, and they come out the same each time (Day 3). He felt watched twice at the field's edge and found nothing (Day 3). He has learned the carter's knot by watching it once and ties it the carter's way (Day 2). In the lane he saw the brake hard down and the rim still turning under the shoe, and put that away because there was no time for it; afterwards he asked the lane whether anyone had looked at the axle (Day 2). His sense has read everyone near him over the three days: Bison at the same shown rank as his own with an unread push beneath it; no qi in Lowen Wray, Pasco Cobb, Kitto Hale, the mill yard, the miller, the child, the wheelwright or the sower; and a faint trace of qi in the basket-weaver (Day 2) and the forester (Day 3). He watched the basket-weaver's face for a second look and did not get one (text revised 2026-10-17).
**Psychological state:** none recorded (no PIS event; the PIS level and the named wounds stay in Section 5)
**Physical state:** Whole at the close of Chapter 008 (Ref: 1.1.1.008); no wound and no mark is stated. Through the day he keeps his shoulders "a hand narrower" and his breath at "a strong man's share"; in the unwatched hour the shoulders come down and the breath "took what it wanted", and he sits on the flat stone beside the post with his hands, "which had held something since before light", open on his knees, and at the end of the hour he is still sitting there with his hands open on his knees. *Earlier, as of Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006):* Whole at the close of Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006); no wound. The Hook Wing's hooks "took hold of the bare skin and could not get in" (Area block 03); his coat is torn from collar to hem, "has gone into the yard in three pieces" and a strip of it still hangs from his belt (Area block 03); in the hall a blanket is round his bare back (Area block 05). After the costly moves his breath is held burning in his chest and his hand shakes against his thigh (***Immovable Stand***, Area blocks 03 and 04), and his chest burns after ***Staggering Blow*** (Area block 04). On Day 17 he draws water and carries a door frame, a roof beam, forty planks and the first of the posts, "each load was a strong man's load and no more" (Area block 06). *Earlier, as of Chapter 002 (Ref: 1.1.1.002):* whole; no wound or mark is stated on the page. The page states no hurt after he stopped the loaded cart with his shoulder and hip (Day 2). On Day 3 he carries the last, big sack of the strip alone.
**Metaphysical state:** At the close of Chapter 008 (Ref: 1.1.1.008) the heat lies banked under the weight "as it had for a year; neither eased and neither pushed". The weight bore down on the heat at Bison's question whether they hold to what they chose, and the stillness came over him "from the hands inward"; the heat "stirred under the weight" as he asked "Do you want it said?" and settled. The weight is named: the Seal, "the binding that lay over the heat in him", first named aloud in the hour ("My father put the Seal on me"); the page does not say it is worn or embodied. The page shows the shown rank (***Champion Level 8 High***) and, once, in his thought, the true level under it (***Demigod Level 1 Mid***), with no step or gap; the Seal is taken by the two men as a few years' work (their belief). His sense, turned out as the guard of the hour, found no one within sight or hearing. *Earlier, as of Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006):* At the close of Chapter 006 (Ref: 1.1.1.006) the weight lies on him again. In the bakery yard it gave "a finger's width and no more" and closed (Area block 03); it gave "a little more than before" after the first ***Immovable Stand*** and closed; at the second in the mill square the weight over the heat gave "nothing at all", and it held him to the one chosen blow of ***Staggering Blow*** while the heat went up under it "as far as it could go" (Area blocks 03 and 04). By dusk on Day 17 "the old weight lay on him again, with the heat banked under it ... The give he had felt in the yard had closed" (Area block 06). The page shows the current level only (***Champion Level 8 High***, in his three sizes, Area block 01); the Seal is not named. *Earlier, as of Chapter 002 (Ref: 1.1.1.002):* the pressure under his calm rose twice and was held to a strong man's measure: at Kitto Hale's cask on Day 1 (the heat from no place he can point to, a flat weight settling over it and pressing down, one breath of stillness, then a strong man's lift, and the weight eased by a small measure; the heat is the thing beneath the Champion, pushing outward, and the weight is what presses back on it) and at the loaded cart on Day 2 (the heat up in him and the weight down on it; he stayed under the weight and gave the cart exactly what a strong man had to give). Beneath his shown rank (***Champion Level 8 High***) something unnamed pushes, steady, held down by the weight; in a year neither has eased (text revised 2026-10-17). The page shows no heat on Day 3.
**Soul state:** none recorded at story start

**Last changed:** Chapter 008 — The Hour Nobody Watches (Ref: 1.1.1.008), the evening of 1247.2.21 / PD 49 (the change is also a Section 10 row, which names this Section); the fields above keep the Chapter 006 and Chapter 002 states after each new line as history. Earlier: Chapter 006 — The Edge of the Forest (Ref: 1.1.1.006), the evening of 1247.2.17 / PD 45. Earlier: Chapter 002 — The Well at First Light (Ref: 1.1.1.002), the morning of 1247.1.3 / PD 3, rewritten for the text revised in place on 2026-10-17.

**Narrative summary (as of Chapter 008, Ref: 1.1.1.008).** On the evening of the twenty-first day of Melorany (1247.2.21 / PD 49) Hercules is on the shelf of high ground above Greenhollow Cross at the head of the log slide, where the village has hung a field bell on a post of new ash and its watch slate has put him and Bison as a pair, in the first dark after the unwatched hour. He sits on the flat stone beside the post with his hands open on his knees, the heat banked under the weight as it has been for a year and his shoulders let down from the narrow carriage of the day. In the hour he has told Bison the silhouette at the forest's edge and the host's three departures, and heard the far ford and the dead lying with their heads to the roofs; the two read the host as sent by someone, with no name and no reason, and agree to keep the watch, the Chief's word and their silence in the lane. He has answered that they hold to what they chose, said the fall in outline, named the Seal aloud for the first time, said he suspects his uncle and his father, set his true level once in his thought and spoken the word "Olympus" once; the pull back to the arrival self did not come. The village does not know: the drover who asked about the burn pit was told last winter's thatch. What Bison is, any old-tongue word, and everything about the fall beyond the outline (the crime, any charge, any victim, the proceeding, home, every name, title, place and polity of the fathers and the uncle) stay unspoken. The ruled evolutions of the hour are Evolution Entries 9 to 12 (Section 14); the daily cover and the daily conversion into labor stand.

**Narrative summary (as of Chapter 006, Ref: 1.1.1.006).** On the evening of the seventeenth day of Melorany (1247.2.17 / PD 45) Hercules is alone at the hedge of Greenhollow Cross in the last of the light, with a woman drawing water at the well within sight and the forest beyond the ditch with its pale lines faint in the bark. He has been seen at full measure: on the night of the fifteenth the village watched from its doors, shutters and the hall's doorway while he took a Hook Wing's hooks across his bare back and, with Bison, killed a host of twenty-eight Warrior beasts with bare-handed Named Moves; the Chief has ruled that what was seen stays in the village, and the village has planned a watch at the forest's edge, a mended hedge, stores for the hall and a bell on a post at the head of the slide. His coat is gone (it went into the bakery yard in three pieces), Lowen Wray's left arm is in a sling, Yestin Hocking walks on a stick and Gwenna Tangye's shoulder is bound; the carcasses burn at the village's pit. The old weight lies on him again with the heat banked under it, he goes at a heavy man's pace, and the reserve is back. He holds the host's conduct and a man who stood at the forest's edge with his hands in his sleeves, found by no sense, as facts he can name and cannot lift, and has told no one; Bison, a long stride off beside him, has seen something of his own and told no one either. The Seal, what Bison is, where the two come from and the old word remain unspoken.

**Narrative summary (as of Chapter 002, Ref: 1.1.1.002).** On the morning of the third day of spring (1247.1.3 / PD 3) Hercules is at the end of his strip in the seed field of Greenhollow Cross, against the hedge, with the ditch and the first trunks of Skyvein Forest beyond it, and the last and heaviest sack of the strip on his shoulders; he is heading along the edge of the field toward the far strips, where the sowers' bags will soon run dry. He has carried and held for three days: the water and Kitto Hale's cask on the first, the mill-yard sacks, the carter's knot and the stopped cart on the second, the seed on the third. The village greets him by his bare first name and looks to him for the heavy end. The cart that slid in the lane stands in the wheelwright's yard on two props with its near wheel off. Bison is on the high ground above the village, where the timber track comes out. Nothing about his body, mind or standing has changed beyond the speech register the author ruled (Evolution Entry 3, Section 14), and the Seal, what Bison is, where the two come from and the old word remain unspoken. In his own mind the shown rank (***Champion Level 8 High***) is the second of three sizes of himself, and the third, an unnamed push beneath it, is held down by a weight that no one has named; his sense has read Bison at the same shown rank and the village as nearly all Unawakened, with a faint trace of qi in the basket-weaver and the forester (***Senior Level 1 Low***), and nobody practises (text revised in place 2026-10-17).
